			local function focusResultEntry(entry)
				local node = entry and entry.Node
				local obj = entry and entry.Obj
				if not node and obj then
					node = nodes[obj]
				end
				if not node or not node.Obj then
					addMessage("Dex", "That result is not focusable in Explorer right now.", defaultMessageColor)
					return false
				end
				Explorer.Window:Show()
				selection:Set(node)
				Explorer.ViewNode(node)
				return true
			end

			local function clearResultButtons()
				for _, child in ipairs(resultsScroll:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("TextButton") or child:IsA("TextLabel") then
						child:Destroy()
					end
				end
				scrollResultsToBottom()
			end

			local updateAssistantUi

			local function refreshResultsPanel()
				clearResultButtons()
				resultsTitle.Text = resultsState.Title or "Results"

				if type(resultsState.Entries) ~= "table" or #resultsState.Entries == 0 then
					local emptyLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
					emptyLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					emptyLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 20)
					emptyLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
					emptyLabel.Text = "No results yet."
					emptyLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 165)
					emptyLabel.TextSize = 13
					emptyLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
					emptyLabel.Parent = resultsScroll
					scrollResultsToBottom()
					return
				end

				for i = 1,#resultsState.Entries do
					local entry = resultsState.Entries[i]
					local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
					button.AutoButtonColor = false
					button.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
					button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 49, 58)
					button.BorderSizePixel = 0
					button.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 0)
					button.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
					button.Text = entry.Label or ("Result " .. i)
					button.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
					button.TextSize = 13
					button.TextWrapped = true
					button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
					button.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
					button.Parent = resultsScroll

					local buttonCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
					buttonCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
					buttonCorner.Parent = button

					local buttonPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
					buttonPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 5)
					buttonPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 5)
					buttonPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
					buttonPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
					buttonPadding.Parent = button

					button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						focusResultEntry(entry)
					end)
				end

				scrollResultsToBottom()
			end

			local function setResultEntries(title, entries)
				resultsState.Title = title or "Results"
				resultsState.Entries = entries or {}
				refreshResultsPanel()
				updateAssistantUi()
			end

			local function buildNodeResultEntries(nodesList, reason, limit)
				local entries = {}
				limit = math.min(#nodesList, limit or #nodesList)
				for i = 1,limit do
					local node = nodesList[i]
					if node and node.Obj then
						local label = ("%d. [%s] %s"):format(i, node.Obj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(node.Obj))
						if reason and reason ~= "" then
							label = label .. "\nvia " .. reason
						end
						entries[#entries + 1] = {
							Node = node,
							Obj = node.Obj,
							Label = label,
						}
					end
				end
				return entries
			end

			local function buildTextResultEntries(results, limit)
				local entries = {}
				limit = math.min(#results, limit or #results)
				for i = 1,limit do
					local entry = results[i]
					if entry and entry.Node and entry.Node.Obj then
						entries[#entries + 1] = {
							Node = entry.Node,
							Obj = entry.Node.Obj,
							Label = ("%d. [%s] %s\nvia %s"):format(
								i,
								entry.Node.Obj.ClassName,
								Explorer.GetInstancePath(entry.Node.Obj),
								table.concat(entry.Hits or {}, ", ")
							),
						}
					end
				end
				return entries
			end

			local function buildScriptResultEntries(matches, snippetMap, limit, includeSnippet)
				local entries = {}
				includeSnippet = includeSnippet ~= false
				limit = math.min(#matches, limit or #matches)
				for i = 1,limit do
					local scr = matches[i]
					local node = scr and nodes[scr]
					if scr then
						local label = ("%d. [%s] %s"):format(i, scr.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(scr))
						if includeSnippet and snippetMap and snippetMap[scr] then
							label = label .. "\n" .. snippetMap[scr]
						end
						entries[#entries + 1] = {
							Node = node,
							Obj = scr,
							Label = label,
						}
					end
				end
				return entries
			end

			local function appendResultEntries(target, moreEntries, maxCount)
				for i = 1,#moreEntries do
					target[#target + 1] = moreEntries[i]
					if maxCount and #target >= maxCount then
						break
					end
				end
			end

			local function addThinkingIntroMessage()
				local meta = getCurrentModelMeta()
				if meta.SupportsThinking then
					addThinkingMessage("Dex", "Thinking summaries show provider thought summaries when available, plus Dex's local action log.", thinkingMessageColor)
				else
					addThinkingMessage("Dex", unsupportedThinkingText, thinkingMessageColor)
				end
			end

			local function setBusy(state)
				requestBusy = state
				sendButton.Text = state and "Wait..." or "Send"
				sendButton.BackgroundColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(48, 48, 48) or Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
			end

			updateAssistantUi = function()
				local providerMeta = getCurrentProviderMeta()
				local modelMeta = getCurrentModelMeta()
				local hasKey = getCurrentApiKey() ~= ""
				local hasRequest = env.request ~= nil
				local usingReal = assistantState.Mode == "real"
				local hasResults = type(resultsState.Entries) == "table" and #resultsState.Entries > 0
				local lookupMode = assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto"
				local lookupReady = env.canAttemptDexDecompile and env.canAttemptDexDecompile(lookupMode)
				local topOffset = usingReal and 126 or 42
				local sideVisible = hasResults or (usingReal and assistantState.ShowThinking)
				local sidePanelWidth = sideVisible and 224 or 0
				local sideGap = sideVisible and 8 or 0
				local bottomInset = 86
				local sendWidth = 80
				local contentWidth = content.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and content.AbsoluteSize.X or 712
				local mainWidth = math.max(220, contentWidth - 8 - sidePanelWidth - sideGap)
				local inputWidth = math.max(120, mainWidth - (sendWidth + 8))
				local keyPageText = providerMeta.KeyUrl:gsub("^https?://", "")

				if not usingReal then
					providerDropdownOpen = false
					modelDropdownOpen = false
				end

				defaultButton.BackgroundColor3 = usingReal and Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55) or Color3.fromRGB(76, 76, 76)
				realButton.BackgroundColor3 = usingReal and Color3.fromRGB(50, 98, 70) or Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55)
				assistantState.LookupButton.BackgroundColor3 = lookupReady and Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55) or Color3.fromRGB(92, 58, 58)
				assistantState.LookupButton.TextColor3 = lookupReady and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 210)
				providerButton.BackgroundColor3 = usingReal and Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
				providerDropdownButton.BackgroundColor3 = usingReal and Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
				modelButton.BackgroundColor3 = usingReal and Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
				modelDropdownButton.BackgroundColor3 = usingReal and Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
				thinkingToggleButton.BackgroundColor3 = assistantState.ShowThinking and Color3.fromRGB(63, 94, 124) or Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55)
				thinkingToggleButton.Text = assistantState.ShowThinking and "Hide thinking" or "Show thinking"

				providerButton.Text = providerMeta.Name
				assistantState.LookupButton.Text = "Lookup: " .. lookupMode
				providerDropdownButton.Text = providerDropdownOpen and "^" or "v"
				modelButton.Text = truncateText(modelMeta.Name, 26)
				modelDropdownButton.Text = modelDropdownOpen and "^" or "v"
				modelLabel.Text = ('Provider: <b>%s</b> | Model: <b>%s</b> <font color="%s">(%s)</font>'):format(providerMeta.Name, modelMeta.Name, modelMeta.TierHex, modelMeta.Tier)
				providerLabel.Text = "Key page: " .. keyPageText

				providerButton.Visible = usingReal
				providerDropdownButton.Visible = usingReal
				modelButton.Visible = usingReal
				modelDropdownButton.Visible = usingReal
				modelLabel.Visible = usingReal
				providerLabel.Visible = usingReal
				statusLabel.Visible = usingReal
				providerDropdownFrame.Visible = usingReal and providerDropdownOpen
				modelDropdownFrame.Visible = usingReal and modelDropdownOpen
				thinkingToggleButton.Visible = usingReal
				keyBox.Visible = usingReal
				saveKeyButton.Visible = usingReal
				copyLinkButton.Visible = usingReal

				if usingReal then
					settingsFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 114)
					chatFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, topOffset)
					chatFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, mainWidth, 1, -(topOffset + bottomInset))
				else
					settingsFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 34)
					chatFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, topOffset)
					chatFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, mainWidth, 1, -(topOffset + bottomInset))
				end

				chipFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -54)
				chipFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, mainWidth, 0, 22)
				inputBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -28)
				inputBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, inputWidth, 0, 22)
				sendButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, sendWidth, 0, 22)
				sendButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12 + inputWidth, 1, -28)

				if hasResults and usingReal and assistantState.ShowThinking then
					resultsFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -228, 0, topOffset)
					resultsFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 224, 0, 152)
					resultsFrame.Visible = true
					thinkingFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -228, 0, topOffset + 160)
					thinkingFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 224, 1, -(topOffset + 246))
					thinkingFrame.Visible = true
				elseif hasResults then
					resultsFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -228, 0, topOffset)
					resultsFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 224, 1, -(topOffset + bottomInset))
					resultsFrame.Visible = true
					thinkingFrame.Visible = false
				elseif usingReal and assistantState.ShowThinking then
					resultsFrame.Visible = false
					thinkingFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -228, 0, topOffset)
					thinkingFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 224, 1, -(topOffset + bottomInset))
					thinkingFrame.Visible = true
				else
					resultsFrame.Visible = false
					thinkingFrame.Visible = false
				end

				for i = 1,#providerOptionRows do
					local row = providerOptionRows[i]
					local selected = row.Meta.Code == providerMeta.Code
					row.Button.BackgroundColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(58, 84, 106) or Color3.fromRGB(42, 42, 42)
					row.NameLabel.TextColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 225)
				end

				for i = 1,#modelOptionRows do
					local row = modelOptionRows[i]
					local selected = row.Meta.Code == modelMeta.Code
					row.Button.BackgroundColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(58, 84, 106) or Color3.fromRGB(42, 42, 42)
					row.NameLabel.TextColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 225)
				end

				if usingReal then
					keyBox.PlaceholderText = "Paste " .. providerMeta.Name .. " API key"
					if not hasRequest then
						statusLabel.Text = ("Real ai needs an executor request/http function first. Script lookup: %s"):format(lookupMode)
						statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 130, 130)
					elseif not hasKey then
						statusLabel.Text = ("%s selected. Paste an API key to use it. Script lookup: %s"):format(providerMeta.Name, lookupMode)
						statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 185, 110)
					elseif modelMeta.Tier == "Paid" then
						statusLabel.Text = ("%s is ready. This selected model may require billing on your account. Script lookup: %s"):format(providerMeta.Name, lookupMode)
						statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 185, 110)
					else
						statusLabel.Text = ("Real ai is ready with %s. Script lookup: %s"):format(providerMeta.Name, lookupMode)
						statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(145, 215, 165)
					end
					inputBox.PlaceholderText = 'Try "help" for commands.'
				else
					statusLabel.Text = ("Dex ai uses Dex's built-in local search plus script and remote summaries. Script lookup: %s"):format(lookupMode)
					statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170)
					inputBox.PlaceholderText = 'Try "help" for commands.'
				end
			end

			local function setAssistantMode(mode, silent)
				if mode ~= "real" and mode ~= "default" then
					return
				end

				assistantState.Mode = mode
				if mode ~= "real" then
					providerDropdownOpen = false
					modelDropdownOpen = false
					clearThinking()
				elseif assistantState.ShowThinking then
					clearThinking()
					addThinkingIntroMessage()
				end
				updateAssistantUi()
				if env.writefile then
					saveAssistantConfig()
				end

				if not silent then
					if mode == "real" then
						addMessage("Dex", "Real ai selected", defaultMessageColor)
					else
						addMessage("Dex", "Dex ai selected", defaultMessageColor)
					end
				end
			end

			local function toggleThinking()
				assistantState.ShowThinking = not assistantState.ShowThinking
				if not assistantState.ShowThinking then
					clearThinking()
				else
					clearThinking()
					addThinkingIntroMessage()
				end
				updateAssistantUi()
				if env.writefile then
					saveAssistantConfig()
				end
			end

			local function refreshProviderDropdownCanvas()
				local sizeY = providerDropdownLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 6
				providerDropdownScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, sizeY)
			end

			providerDropdownLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(refreshProviderDropdownCanvas)

			local function refreshModelDropdownCanvas()
				local sizeY = modelDropdownLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 6
				modelDropdownScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, sizeY)
			end

			modelDropdownLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(refreshModelDropdownCanvas)

			local function setSelectedProvider(code, silent)
				syncApiKeyFromInput()
				local provider = getProviderMeta(code)
				assistantState.ProviderCode = provider.Code
				assistantState.ModelCode = getFirstModelForProvider(provider.Code).Code
				assistantState.ApiKey = getCurrentApiKey()
				providerDropdownOpen = false
				modelDropdownOpen = false
				if assistantState.Mode == "real" and assistantState.ShowThinking then
					clearThinking()
					addThinkingIntroMessage()
				end
				populateModelDropdown()
				if keyBox then
					keyBox.Text = getCurrentApiKey()
				end
				updateAssistantUi()
				if env.writefile then
					saveAssistantConfig()
				end
				if not silent then
					addMessage("Dex", ("Real ai provider set to %s."):format(provider.Name), defaultMessageColor)
				end
			end

			local function setSelectedModel(code, silent)
				syncApiKeyFromInput()
				local meta = getModelMeta(code)
				assistantState.ModelCode = meta.Code
				modelDropdownOpen = false
				if assistantState.Mode == "real" and assistantState.ShowThinking then
					clearThinking()
					addThinkingIntroMessage()
				end
				updateAssistantUi()
				if env.writefile then
					saveAssistantConfig()
				end
				if not silent then
					addMessage("Dex", ("Real ai model set to %s (%s)."):format(meta.Name, meta.Tier), defaultMessageColor)
				end
			end

			local function populateProviderDropdown()
				providerOptionRows = {}

				for i = 1,#aiProviders do
					local meta = aiProviders[i]
					local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
					button.AutoButtonColor = false
					button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(42, 42, 42)
					button.BorderSizePixel = 0
					button.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 28)
					button.Text = ""
					button.ZIndex = 22
					button.Parent = providerDropdownScroll

					local buttonCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
					buttonCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
					buttonCorner.Parent = button

					local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
					nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
					nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0)
					nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
					nameLabel.Text = meta.Name
					nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 225)
					nameLabel.TextSize = 13
					nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
					nameLabel.ZIndex = 23
					nameLabel.Parent = button

					button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						setSelectedProvider(meta.Code, true)
					end)

					providerOptionRows[#providerOptionRows + 1] = {
						Meta = meta,
						Button = button,
						NameLabel = nameLabel,
					}
				end

				refreshProviderDropdownCanvas()
			end

			populateModelDropdown = function()
				for _, child in ipairs(modelDropdownScroll:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("TextButton") then
						child:Destroy()
					end
				end
				modelOptionRows = {}
				local models = getProviderModels(assistantState.ProviderCode)

				for i = 1,#models do
					local meta = models[i]
					local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
					button.AutoButtonColor = false
					button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(42, 42, 42)
					button.BorderSizePixel = 0
					button.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 28)
					button.Text = ""
					button.ZIndex = 22
					button.Parent = modelDropdownScroll

					local buttonCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
					buttonCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
					buttonCorner.Parent = button

					local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
					nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
					nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -86, 1, 0)
					nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
					nameLabel.Text = meta.Name
					nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 225)
					nameLabel.TextSize = 13
					nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
					nameLabel.ZIndex = 23
					nameLabel.Parent = button

					local badgeLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
					badgeLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					badgeLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 0)
					badgeLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 1, 0)
					badgeLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
					badgeLabel.Text = "(" .. meta.Tier .. ")"
					badgeLabel.TextColor3 = meta.TierColor
					badgeLabel.TextSize = 12
					badgeLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
					badgeLabel.ZIndex = 23
					badgeLabel.Parent = button

					button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						setSelectedModel(meta.Code, true)
					end)

					modelOptionRows[#modelOptionRows + 1] = {
						Meta = meta,
						Button = button,
						NameLabel = nameLabel,
						BadgeLabel = badgeLabel,
					}
				end

				refreshModelDropdownCanvas()
			end

			local function saveKeyWithFeedback()
				syncApiKeyFromInput()
				local success, err = saveAssistantConfig()
				if success then
					addMessage("Dex", "Explorer AI settings saved.", defaultMessageColor)
				else
					addMessage("Dex", err, defaultMessageColor)
				end
				updateAssistantUi()
			end

			defaultButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				providerDropdownOpen = false
				modelDropdownOpen = false
				setAssistantMode("default")
			end)

			realButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				providerDropdownOpen = false
				modelDropdownOpen = false
				setAssistantMode("real")
			end)

			assistantState.LookupButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local mode = assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto"
				if mode == "Auto" then
					mode = "Executor"
				elseif mode == "Executor" then
					mode = "Local"
				else
					mode = "Auto"
				end
				assistantState.LookupMode = mode
				updateAssistantUi()
				if env.writefile then
					saveAssistantConfig()
				end
				addMessage("Dex", ("Explorer AI script lookup set to %s. Local is usually the faster option for huge script scans when it is available."):format(mode), defaultMessageColor)
			end)

			providerButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				if assistantState.Mode ~= "real" then
					setAssistantMode("real", true)
				end
				modelDropdownOpen = false
				providerDropdownOpen = not providerDropdownOpen
				updateAssistantUi()
			end)

			providerDropdownButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				if assistantState.Mode ~= "real" then
					setAssistantMode("real", true)
				end
				modelDropdownOpen = false
				providerDropdownOpen = not providerDropdownOpen
				updateAssistantUi()
			end)

			modelDropdownButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				if assistantState.Mode ~= "real" then
					setAssistantMode("real", true)
				end
				providerDropdownOpen = false
				modelDropdownOpen = not modelDropdownOpen
				updateAssistantUi()
			end)

			modelButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				if assistantState.Mode ~= "real" then
					setAssistantMode("real", true)
				end
				providerDropdownOpen = false
				modelDropdownOpen = not modelDropdownOpen
				updateAssistantUi()
			end)

			thinkingToggleButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleThinking)

			saveKeyButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(saveKeyWithFeedback)

			copyLinkButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local providerMeta = getCurrentProviderMeta()
				if env.setclipboard then
					local success, err = pcall(env.setclipboard, providerMeta.KeyUrl)
					if success then
						addMessage("Dex", ("Copied the %s key page URL to your clipboard."):format(providerMeta.Name), defaultMessageColor)
					else
						addMessage("Dex", ("I could not copy the URL: %s"):format(tostring(err)), defaultMessageColor)
					end
				else
					addMessage("Dex", ("Open this for %s API keys: %s"):format(providerMeta.Name, providerMeta.KeyUrl), defaultMessageColor)
				end
			end)

			keyBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
				syncApiKeyFromInput()
				if env.writefile then
					saveAssistantConfig()
				end
				updateAssistantUi()
				if enterPressed then
					saveKeyWithFeedback()
				end
			end)

				syncApiKeyFromInput()
				updateAssistantUi()
			end)

			populateProviderDropdown()
			populateModelDropdown()

			safeIsA = function(obj, className)
				if not obj then
					return false
				end
				local success, result = pcall(obj.IsA, obj, className)
				return success and result
			end

			focusNode = function(node)
				if not node then return false end
				Explorer.Window:Show()
				selection:Set(node)
				Explorer.ViewNode(node)
				return true
			end

			local function walkNodes(root, visitor)
				root = root or nodes[game]
				if not root then return end

				for i = 1,#root do
					local node = root[i]
					if not node.Del and node.Obj then
						if visitor(node.Obj, node) then
							return true
						end

						if #node > 0 and walkNodes(node, visitor) then
							return true
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local function getSelectedNodes()
				return (selection and selection.List) or {}
			end

			local function getSelectedPrimaryNode()
				local list = getSelectedNodes()
				return list[1]
			end

			local function isViableScript(obj)
				return obj and safeIsA(obj, "LuaSourceContainer") and env.isViableDecompileScript and env.isViableDecompileScript(obj)
			end

			assistantState.IsLikelyRobloxDefaultScript = function(obj)
				if not obj then
					return false
				end

				local current = obj
				local depth = 0
				while current and depth < 32 do
					local currentName = tostring(current.Name or "")
					if currentName == "PlayerModule" or currentName == "RobloxGui" or currentName == "CoreScripts" then
						return true
					end

					local lockedSuccess, isLocked = pcall(function()
						return current.RobloxLocked
					end)
					if lockedSuccess and isLocked then
						return true
					end

					local parent = current.Parent
					if parent == game then
						return currentName == "CoreGui" or currentName == "CorePackages" or currentName == "Chat"
					end

					current = parent
					depth = depth + 1
				end

				return false
			end

			local function findScriptInNode(node)
				if not node or node.Del or not node.Obj then return nil end
				if isViableScript(node.Obj) then
					return node
				end

				for i = 1,#node do
					local found = findScriptInNode(node[i])
					if found then
						return found
					end
				end
			end

			local function getSelectedScriptNode()
				local selected = getSelectedNodes()
				for i = 1,#selected do
					if isViableScript(selected[i].Obj) then
						return selected[i], selected[i]
					end
				end

				for i = 1,#selected do
					local found = findScriptInNode(selected[i])
					if found then
						return found, selected[i]
					end
				end
			end

			local function isRemoteObject(obj)
				return obj and (
					safeIsA(obj, "RemoteEvent")
					or safeIsA(obj, "RemoteFunction")
					or safeIsA(obj, "UnreliableRemoteEvent")
					or safeIsA(obj, "BindableEvent")
					or safeIsA(obj, "BindableFunction")
				)
			end

			local function findRemoteInNode(node)
				if not node or node.Del or not node.Obj then return nil end
				if isRemoteObject(node.Obj) then
					return node
				end

				for i = 1,#node do
					local found = findRemoteInNode(node[i])
					if found then
						return found
					end
				end
			end

			local function getSelectedRemoteNode()
				local selected = getSelectedNodes()
				for i = 1,#selected do
					if isRemoteObject(selected[i].Obj) then
						return selected[i], selected[i]
					end
				end

				for i = 1,#selected do
					local found = findRemoteInNode(selected[i])
					if found then
						return found, selected[i]
					end
				end
			end

			local gatherSearchableScripts
			local searchScriptsByText

			assistantState.ConnectedScriptGenericNames = {
				["attachment"] = true,
				["billboardgui"] = true,
				["bindableevent"] = true,
				["bindablefunction"] = true,
				["button"] = true,
				["clickdetector"] = true,
				["event"] = true,
				["folder"] = true,
				["frame"] = true,
				["function"] = true,
				["gui"] = true,
				["holder"] = true,
				["imagebutton"] = true,
				["imagelabel"] = true,
				["label"] = true,
				["localscript"] = true,
				["meshpart"] = true,
				["model"] = true,
				["module"] = true,
				["modulescript"] = true,
				["part"] = true,
				["proximityprompt"] = true,
				["remote"] = true,
				["remoteevent"] = true,
				["remotefunction"] = true,
				["screen"] = true,
				["screengui"] = true,
				["script"] = true,
				["surfacegui"] = true,
				["text"] = true,
				["textbox"] = true,
				["textbutton"] = true,
				["textlabel"] = true,
				["trigger"] = true,
				["ui"] = true,
				["unreliableremoteevent"] = true,
				["value"] = true,
			}

			assistantState.ConnectedScriptGenericContainers = {
				["workspace"] = true,
				["players"] = true,
				["playergui"] = true,
				["replicatedstorage"] = true,
				["replicatedfirst"] = true,
				["startergui"] = true,
				["starterpack"] = true,
				["starterplayer"] = true,
				["starterplayerscripts"] = true,
				["startercharacterscripts"] = true,
				["serverstorage"] = true,
				["serverscriptservice"] = true,
				["lighting"] = true,
				["soundservice"] = true,
				["chat"] = true,
				["coregui"] = true,
				["corepackages"] = true,
				["game"] = true,
			}

			assistantState.IsGenericConnectedName = function(name, className)
				name = trim(name)
				local loweredName = lower(name)
				if loweredName == "" then
					return true
				end
				if #loweredName <= 2 then
					return true
				end
				if className and loweredName == lower(className) then
					return true
				end
				if assistantState.ConnectedScriptGenericNames[loweredName] or assistantState.ConnectedScriptGenericContainers[loweredName] then
					return true
				end
				return false
			end

			assistantState.PushConnectedSearchTerm = function(target, seen, value, weight, label)
				value = trim(value)
				if value == "" then
					return
				end

				local loweredValue = lower(value)
				if seen[loweredValue] then
					return
				end
				seen[loweredValue] = true
				target[#target + 1] = {
					Value = value,
					Weight = weight or 1,
					Label = label or value,
				}
			end

			assistantState.CollectConnectedSearchTerms = function(node)
				local terms = {}
				local seen = {}
				local obj = node and node.Obj
				if not obj then
					return terms
				end

				local objName = tostring(obj.Name or "")
				local objGeneric = assistantState.IsGenericConnectedName(objName, obj.ClassName)
				assistantState.PushConnectedSearchTerm(terms, seen, objName, objGeneric and 6 or 10, objGeneric and "object name (generic)" or "object name")

				if safeIsA(obj, "BillboardGui") or safeIsA(obj, "SurfaceGui") then
					local success, adornee = pcall(function()
						return obj.Adornee
					end)
					if success and adornee and adornee ~= obj then
						assistantState.PushConnectedSearchTerm(terms, seen, tostring(adornee.Name or ""), 8, "adornee name")
						if adornee.Parent and adornee.Parent ~= game then
							assistantState.PushConnectedSearchTerm(terms, seen, tostring(adornee.Parent.Name or ""), 5, "adornee parent")
						end
					end
				end

				local current = obj.Parent
				local depth = 1
				while current and current ~= game and depth <= 3 do
					local currentName = tostring(current.Name or "")
					local currentGeneric = assistantState.IsGenericConnectedName(currentName, current.ClassName)
					if not currentGeneric or (#terms <= 1 and depth <= 2) then
						local weight = depth == 1 and 7 or (depth == 2 and 5 or 3)
						assistantState.PushConnectedSearchTerm(terms, seen, currentName, weight, depth == 1 and "parent name" or (depth == 2 and "grandparent name" or "ancestor name"))
					end
					current = current.Parent
					depth = depth + 1
				end

				return terms
			end

			assistantState.GetCachedLoweredScriptSource = function(scr, source, cacheKey)
				local loweredBucket = assistantState.ScriptSourceLowerCache[scr]
				if type(loweredBucket) ~= "table" then
					loweredBucket = {}
					assistantState.ScriptSourceLowerCache[scr] = loweredBucket
				end

				local cachedLowered = loweredBucket[cacheKey]
				if type(cachedLowered) == "string" then
					return cachedLowered
				end

				cachedLowered = lower(source)
				loweredBucket[cacheKey] = cachedLowered
				return cachedLowered
			end

			assistantState.SearchScriptsConnectedToNode = function(node, options)
				options = options or {}
				if not node or not node.Obj then
					return {}, "Nothing is selected in Explorer right now.", {}, 0, 0, 0, 0, {}, 0, false, 0
				end

				local terms = assistantState.CollectConnectedSearchTerms(node)
				if #terms == 0 then
					return {}, "DEX could not build any useful search terms for the selected object.", {}, 0, 0, 0, 0, {}, 0, false, 0
				end

				local primaryTerm = terms[1]
				local matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount, failureDetails, undisclosedFailureCount = searchScriptsByText(primaryTerm.Value, {
					snippetLimit = 0,
					progress = options.progress,
					yieldEvery = options.yieldEvery or 25,
				})
				if err then
					return {}, err, terms, scannedCount, failedCount, totalScripts, skippedDefaultCount, failureDetails, elapsedSeconds, truncated, undisclosedFailureCount or 0
				end

				local lookupMode = assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto"
				local cacheKey = lower(lookupMode)
				local scripts = assistantState.SearchableScriptsCache.List
				if type(scripts) ~= "table" then
					scripts = select(1, gatherSearchableScripts())
				end

				local candidates = {}
				local candidateMap = {}

				local function touchCandidate(scr, termMeta)
					if not scr then
						return
					end

					local record = candidateMap[scr]
					if not record then
						record = {
							Script = scr,
							Score = 0,
							Terms = {},
							SeenTerms = {},
						}
						candidateMap[scr] = record
						candidates[#candidates + 1] = record
					end

					record.Score = record.Score + (termMeta.Weight or 1)
					if not record.SeenTerms[termMeta.Value] then
						record.SeenTerms[termMeta.Value] = true
						record.Terms[#record.Terms + 1] = termMeta.Value
					end
				end

				for i = 1,#matches do
					touchCandidate(matches[i], primaryTerm)
				end

				for termIndex = 2,#terms do
					local termMeta = terms[termIndex]
					local loweredNeedle = lower(termMeta.Value)
					for scriptIndex = 1,#scripts do
						local scr = scripts[scriptIndex]
						local cacheEntry = scriptSourceCache[scr]
						local source = type(cacheEntry) == "table" and cacheEntry[cacheKey] or nil
						if type(source) == "string" and source ~= "" then
							local failureReason = assistantState.GetDecompilerFailureReason and assistantState.GetDecompilerFailureReason(source)
							if not failureReason and assistantState.GetCachedLoweredScriptSource(scr, source, cacheKey):find(loweredNeedle, 1, true) then
								touchCandidate(scr, termMeta)
							end
						end
					end
					task.wait()
				end

				table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
					if a.Score ~= b.Score then
						return a.Score > b.Score
					end
					return Explorer.GetInstancePath(a.Script) < Explorer.GetInstancePath(b.Script)
				end)

				return candidates, nil, terms, scannedCount, failedCount, totalScripts, skippedDefaultCount, failureDetails, elapsedSeconds, truncated, undisclosedFailureCount or 0
			end

			assistantState.BuildConnectedScriptEntries = function(candidateRecords, limit)
				local entries = {}
				limit = math.min(#candidateRecords, limit or #candidateRecords)
				for i = 1,limit do
					local record = candidateRecords[i]
					local scr = record and record.Script
					if scr then
						entries[#entries + 1] = {
							Node = nodes[scr],
							Obj = scr,
							Label = ("%d. [%s] %s\nvia %s"):format(
								i,
								scr.ClassName,
								Explorer.GetInstancePath(scr),
								table.concat(record.Terms or {}, ", ")
							),
						}
					end
				end
				return entries
			end

			assistantState.BuildConnectedScriptsLocalText = function(node, candidateRecords, terms, scannedCount, failedCount, err, totalScripts, skippedDefaultCount, elapsedSeconds, truncated)
				local obj = node.Obj
				local lines = {
					("Selected object: [%s] %s"):format(obj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(obj)),
				}
				local termLabels = {}
				for i = 1,#terms do
					termLabels[#termLabels + 1] = ('"%s"'):format(terms[i].Value)
				end
				if #termLabels > 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "Search terms: " .. table.concat(termLabels, ", ")
				end

				if err then
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Connected-script scan failed: %s"):format(err)
					return table.concat(lines, "\n")
				end

				if #candidateRecords == 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "I did not find any likely connected scripts for that object."
				else
					lines[#lines + 1] = ('I found %d possible connected script%s. Use the results list to open the best candidate.'):format(
						#candidateRecords,
						#candidateRecords == 1 and "" or "s"
					)
					local previewLines = {}
					for i = 1,math.min(#candidateRecords, 3) do
						local record = candidateRecords[i]
						local scr = record.Script
						previewLines[#previewLines + 1] = ("%d. [%s] %s"):format(i, scr.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(scr))
						previewLines[#previewLines + 1] = "   via " .. table.concat(record.Terms or {}, ", ")
					end
					if #previewLines > 0 then
						lines[#lines + 1] = table.concat(previewLines, "\n")
					end
				end

				lines[#lines + 1] = ("Scanned: %d/%d | Failed decompiles: %d"):format(scannedCount or 0, totalScripts or 0, failedCount or 0)
				if (skippedDefaultCount or 0) > 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Skipped Roblox/default scripts: %d"):format(skippedDefaultCount)
				end
				if truncated then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "The scan ended early before every candidate hint could be ranked."
				end
				if type(elapsedSeconds) == "number" and elapsedSeconds > 0 then
					local formattedSeconds = elapsedSeconds >= 10 and ("%.1fs"):format(elapsedSeconds) or ("%.2fs"):format(elapsedSeconds)
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Finished in %s using %s lookup."):format(formattedSeconds, assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto")
				end

				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			assistantState.AddDecompilerFailureReportButton = function(reportTitle, failedCount, failureDetails, undisclosedFailureCount)
				if failedCount <= 0 then
					return
				end
				assistantState.AddChatActionButton(("Open debug report (%d failed)"):format(failedCount), function()
					local lines = {}
					if type(failureDetails) == "table" then
						for failureIndex = 1,#failureDetails do
							local failure = failureDetails[failureIndex]
							local scr = failure and failure.Script
							lines[#lines + 1] = ("%d. [%s] %s"):format(
								failureIndex,
								scr and scr.ClassName or "Script",
								scr and Explorer.GetInstancePath(scr) or "<unknown script>"
							)
							lines[#lines + 1] = ("   Reason: %s"):format(tostring(failure and failure.Reason or "The local decompiler failed without an error message."))
							lines[#lines + 1] = ""
						end
					end
					if (undisclosedFailureCount or 0) > 0 then
						lines[#lines + 1] = ("Plus %d more failed decompile(s) that did not include per-script detail."):format(undisclosedFailureCount)
					end
					if #lines == 0 then
						lines[1] = "DEX counted failed decompiles here, but did not receive per-script detail for them."
					end
					if assistantState.ShowDebugReport then
						assistantState.ShowDebugReport(reportTitle or "Failed decompiles", table.concat(lines, "\n"))
					end
				end, Color3.fromRGB(84, 62, 46))
			end

			local function getSelectedSummary()
				local primary = getSelectedPrimaryNode()
				if not primary or not primary.Obj then
					return "Nothing is selected in Explorer right now."
				end

				return ("Selected: [%s] %s"):format(primary.Obj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(primary.Obj))
			end

			local function getScriptSource(scr)
				local lookupMode = assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto"
				if not env.canAttemptDexDecompile or not env.canAttemptDexDecompile(lookupMode) then
					local mode = lookupMode
					return nil, ("No decompiler is available for Script Viewer mode \"%s\"."):format(mode)
				end
				if not isViableScript(scr) then
					return nil, "That selected object is not a decompilable script."
				end

				local cacheKey = lower(lookupMode)
				local cacheEntry = scriptSourceCache[scr]
				local cached = type(cacheEntry) == "table" and cacheEntry[cacheKey] or nil
				if cached then
					local repaired = env.repairPossibleMojibake and env.repairPossibleMojibake(cached) or cached
					if repaired ~= cached then
						cacheEntry[cacheKey] = repaired
						return repaired
					end
					return cached
				end

				local result, err = (env.dexDecompileScriptInMode or env.dexDecompileScript)(scr, lookupMode)
				if type(result) == "string" and #result > 0 then
					result = env.repairPossibleMojibake and env.repairPossibleMojibake(result) or result
					if type(cacheEntry) ~= "table" then
						cacheEntry = {}
						scriptSourceCache[scr] = cacheEntry
					end
					cacheEntry[cacheKey] = result
					return result
				end

				return nil, ("DEX failed to decompile %s.\n%s"):format(scr.ClassName, tostring(err))
			end

			local function cleanNeedle(str)
				str = trim(str)
				str = str:gsub("^the%s+word%s+", "")
				str = str:gsub("^the%s+text%s+", "")
				str = str:gsub("^word%s+", "")
				str = str:gsub("^text%s+", "")
				str = str:gsub("^named%s+", "")
				str = str:gsub("^called%s+", "")
				str = str:gsub("%s+and%s+show.-$", "")
				str = str:gsub("%s+and%s+select.-$", "")
				str = str:gsub("%s+show%s+me.-$", "")
				str = str:gsub("%s+in%s+the%s+explorer.*$", "")
				str = str:gsub("%s+and%s+stuff$", "")
				str = str:gsub("%s+and%s+things?$", "")
				str = str:gsub("[%?%!%.]+$", "")
				return trim(str)
			end

			local function collectQuotedTerms(message)
				local terms, seen = {}, {}
				for match in message:gmatch('"([^"]+)"') do
					pushUnique(terms, seen, cleanNeedle(match))
				end
				for match in message:gmatch("'([^']+)'") do
					pushUnique(terms, seen, cleanNeedle(match))
				end
				return terms
			end

			local function splitNeedleList(raw)
				raw = cleanNeedle(raw)
				if raw == "" then return {} end

				local terms, seen = {}, {}
				raw = raw:gsub("%s+or%s+", ",")
				raw = raw:gsub("%s+and%s+", ",")

				for piece in raw:gmatch("[^,]+") do
					piece = cleanNeedle(piece)
					local loweredPiece = lower(piece)
					if piece ~= "" and loweredPiece ~= "stuff" and loweredPiece ~= "thing" and loweredPiece ~= "things" then
						pushUnique(terms, seen, piece)
					end
				end

				return terms
			end

			local function extractNeedles(message, patterns, fallback)
				local quoted = collectQuotedTerms(message)
				if #quoted > 0 then
					return quoted
				end

				for i = 1,#patterns do
					local value = message:match(patterns[i])
					if value and cleanNeedle(value) ~= "" then
						return splitNeedleList(value)
					end
				end

				if fallback then
					local value = fallback(message)
					if value and cleanNeedle(value) ~= "" then
						return splitNeedleList(value)
					end
				end

				return {}
			end

			local function resolveKind(lowered)
				local kinds = {
					{"localscript", "LocalScript", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "LocalScript") end},
					{"module script", "ModuleScript", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "ModuleScript") end},
					{"modulescript", "ModuleScript", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "ModuleScript") end},
					{"server script", "Script", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "Script") end},
					{"textbutton", "TextButton", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "TextButton") end},
					{"textlabel", "TextLabel", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "TextLabel") end},
					{"textbox", "TextBox", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "TextBox") end},
					{"imagebutton", "ImageButton", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "ImageButton") end},
					{"imagelabel", "ImageLabel", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "ImageLabel") end},
					{"proximityprompt", "ProximityPrompt", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "ProximityPrompt") end},
					{"proximity prompt", "ProximityPrompt", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "ProximityPrompt") end},
					{"screengui", "ScreenGui", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "ScreenGui") end},
					{"screen gui", "ScreenGui", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "ScreenGui") end},
					{"surfacegui", "SurfaceGui", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "SurfaceGui") end},
					{"surface gui", "SurfaceGui", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "SurfaceGui") end},
					{"billboardgui", "BillboardGui", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "BillboardGui") end},
					{"billboard gui", "BillboardGui", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "BillboardGui") end},
					{"script", "script", function(obj)
						return safeIsA(obj, "Script") or safeIsA(obj, "LocalScript") or safeIsA(obj, "ModuleScript")
					end},
					{"gui", "GUI", function(obj)
						return safeIsA(obj, "GuiBase2d") or safeIsA(obj, "LayerCollector")
					end},
					{"part", "part", function(obj) return safeIsA(obj, "BasePart") end},
					{"remote", "remote", function(obj) return isRemoteObject(obj) end},
				}

				for i = 1,#kinds do
					if lowered:find(kinds[i][1], 1, true) then
						return kinds[i][2], kinds[i][3]
					end
				end

				if lowered:match("%f[%a]ui%f[%A]") then
					return "GUI", function(obj)
						return safeIsA(obj, "GuiBase2d") or safeIsA(obj, "LayerCollector")
					end
				end

				return "instance", function() return true end
			end

			local searchableTextProps = {
				"Name",
				"Text",
				"PlaceholderText",
				"Title",
				"ActionText",
				"ObjectText",
				"Value",
				"ToolTip",
				"Description",
				"DisplayName",
			}

			local function searchByName(term, kindFunc, maxCount)
				local loweredNeedle = lower(term)
				local looseNeedle = normalizeLoose(term)
				local exact, partial = {}, {}

				walkNodes(nil, function(obj, node)
					if isDexObject(obj) or not kindFunc(obj) then
						return false
					end

					local objName = tostring(obj.Name or "")
					local loweredName = lower(objName)
					local looseName = normalizeLoose(objName)

					if loweredName == loweredNeedle or looseName == looseNeedle then
						exact[#exact + 1] = node
					elseif loweredName:find(loweredNeedle, 1, true) or (looseNeedle ~= "" and looseName:find(looseNeedle, 1, true)) then
						partial[#partial + 1] = node
					end

					return maxCount and (#exact + #partial) >= maxCount
				end)

				local results = {}
				for i = 1,#exact do results[#results + 1] = exact[i] end
				for i = 1,#partial do results[#results + 1] = partial[i] end
				return results
			end

			local function getTextPropertyHits(obj, term)
				local loweredNeedle = lower(term)
				local looseNeedle = normalizeLoose(term)
				local hits = {}

				for i = 1,#searchableTextProps do
					local prop = searchableTextProps[i]
					local success, value = pcall(function()
						return obj[prop]
					end)

					if success and type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then
						local loweredValue = lower(value)
						local looseValue = normalizeLoose(value)
						if loweredValue:find(loweredNeedle, 1, true) or (looseNeedle ~= "" and looseValue:find(looseNeedle, 1, true)) then
							hits[#hits + 1] = ("%s=\"%s\""):format(prop, truncateText(value, 48))
						end
					end
				end

				return hits
			end

			local function searchByTextProperties(term, maxCount)
				local results = {}
				walkNodes(nil, function(obj, node)
					if isDexObject(obj) then
						return false
					end

					local hits = getTextPropertyHits(obj, term)
					if #hits > 0 then
						results[#results + 1] = {Node = node, Hits = hits}
						return maxCount and #results >= maxCount
					end
				end)

				return results
			end

			local function summarizeNodes(results, limit)
				local lines = {}
				limit = math.min(#results, limit or 3)

				for i = 1,limit do
					local obj = results[i].Obj
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("%d. [%s] %s"):format(i, obj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(obj))
				end

				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			local function summarizeTextResults(results, limit)
				local lines = {}
				limit = math.min(#results, limit or 3)

				for i = 1,limit do
					local entry = results[i]
					local obj = entry.Node.Obj
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("%d. [%s] %s"):format(i, obj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(obj))
					lines[#lines + 1] = "   via " .. table.concat(entry.Hits, ", ")
				end

				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			local function summarizeScriptObjects(matches, limit)
				local lines = {}
				limit = math.min(#matches, limit or 3)

				for i = 1,limit do
					local scr = matches[i]
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("%d. [%s] %s"):format(i, scr.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(scr))
				end

				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			gatherSearchableScripts = function()
				local now = os.clock()
				if assistantState.SearchableScriptsCache.List and (now - assistantState.SearchableScriptsCache.Stamp) <= 8 then
					return assistantState.SearchableScriptsCache.List, assistantState.SearchableScriptsCache.SkippedDefaultCount or 0
				end

				local scripts = {}
				local skippedDefaultCount = 0
				local seen = {}

				local function addIfViable(obj)
					if not isViableScript(obj) or seen[obj] or isDexObject(obj) then return end
					seen[obj] = true
					if assistantState.IsLikelyRobloxDefaultScript and assistantState.IsLikelyRobloxDefaultScript(obj) then
						skippedDefaultCount = skippedDefaultCount + 1
						return
					end
					scripts[#scripts + 1] = obj
				end

				for _, obj in ipairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
					addIfViable(obj)
				end

				if env.getnilinstances then
					local success, nilInstances = pcall(env.getnilinstances)
					if success and type(nilInstances) == "table" then
						for _, obj in ipairs(nilInstances) do
							addIfViable(obj)
						end
					end
				end

				assistantState.SearchableScriptsCache.List = scripts
				assistantState.SearchableScriptsCache.SkippedDefaultCount = skippedDefaultCount
				assistantState.SearchableScriptsCache.Stamp = now
				return scripts, skippedDefaultCount
			end

			local function getSourceSnippet(source, term, radius)
				if type(source) ~= "string" then return nil end
				local loweredSource = lower(source)
				local loweredTerm = lower(term)
				local startPos = loweredSource:find(loweredTerm, 1, true)
				if not startPos then return nil end

				radius = radius or 72
				local finishPos = startPos + #term - 1
				local snippetStart = math.max(1, startPos - radius)
				local snippetEnd = math.min(#source, finishPos + radius)
				local snippet = source:sub(snippetStart, snippetEnd)
				snippet = snippet:gsub("[\r\n]+", " ")
				snippet = snippet:gsub("%s+", " ")
				snippet = trim(snippet)
				if snippetStart > 1 then
					snippet = "..." .. snippet
				end
				if snippetEnd < #source then
					snippet = snippet .. "..."
				end
				return truncateText(snippet, 180)
			end

			local function getDexLocalSearchUrl()
				if not env.request or not env.getDexLocalDecompilerUrl or not env.getscriptbytecode or not env.base64encode then
					return nil
				end

				local requestUrl = env.getDexLocalDecompilerUrl()
				if type(requestUrl) ~= "string" or requestUrl == "" then
					return nil
				end

				local searchUrl = requestUrl:gsub("/decompile$", "/search-text")
				if searchUrl == requestUrl then
					return nil
				end

				return searchUrl
			end

			local function getSearchResponseStatusAndBody(response)
				if type(response) ~= "table" then
					return nil, nil
				end

				return response.StatusCode or response.Status or response.Code,
					response.Body or response.body or response.ResponseBody or response.responseBody or response.Content
			end

			local DEX_LOCAL_SEARCH_MAX_BYTECODE_BYTES = 100 * 1024
			local function getEncodedScriptBytecode(scr)
				local cached = assistantState.ScriptBytecodeCache[scr]
				if type(cached) == "string" and cached ~= "" then
					return cached
				end

				local success, bytecode = pcall(env.getscriptbytecode, scr)
				if not success or type(bytecode) ~= "string" or bytecode == "" then
					return nil
				end
				if #bytecode > DEX_LOCAL_SEARCH_MAX_BYTECODE_BYTES then
					return nil, ("DEX skipped this script during background search because its bytecode is %d KB. Open it directly to decompile it."):format(math.ceil(#bytecode / 1024))
				end

				local encodeSuccess, encodedBytecode = pcall(env.base64encode, bytecode)
				if not encodeSuccess or type(encodedBytecode) ~= "string" or encodedBytecode == "" then
					return nil
				end

				assistantState.ScriptBytecodeCache[scr] = encodedBytecode
				return encodedBytecode
			end

			searchScriptsByText = function(term, options)
				options = options or {}
				local lookupMode = assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto"
				if not env.canAttemptDexDecompile or not env.canAttemptDexDecompile(lookupMode) then
					local mode = lookupMode
					return {}, 0, 0, ("No decompiler is available for Script Viewer mode \"%s\"."):format(mode), false, 0, {}, 0, 0
				end

				local startedAt = os.clock()
				local loweredNeedle = lower(term)
				local scripts, skippedDefaultCount = gatherSearchableScripts()
				local matches = {}
				local scannedCount, completedCount, failedCount = 0, 0, 0
				local failureDetails = {}
				local snippetMap = {}
				local snippetLimit = options.snippetLimit or 0
				local maxMatches = options.maxMatches
				local maxScans = options.maxScans
				local progressCallback = options.progress
				local truncated = false
				local cacheKey = lower(lookupMode)
				local yieldEvery = options.yieldEvery or 25
				local rawMatches = {}
				local searchToken = env.getDexScriptSearchToken and env.getDexScriptSearchToken() or 0
				local lastProgressAt = 0

				local function reportProgress(force)
					if not progressCallback then
						return
					end
					local now = os.clock()
					if not force and lastProgressAt > 0 and (now - lastProgressAt) < 0.08 then
						return
					end
					lastProgressAt = now
					pcall(progressCallback, completedCount, #scripts, skippedDefaultCount or 0)
				end

				reportProgress(true)
				if progressCallback then
					task.wait()
				end

				local function getLoweredSource(scr, source)
					local loweredBucket = assistantState.ScriptSourceLowerCache[scr]
					if type(loweredBucket) ~= "table" then
						loweredBucket = {}
						assistantState.ScriptSourceLowerCache[scr] = loweredBucket
					end

					local cachedLowered = loweredBucket[cacheKey]
					if type(cachedLowered) == "string" then
						return cachedLowered
					end

					cachedLowered = lower(source)
					loweredBucket[cacheKey] = cachedLowered
					return cachedLowered
				end

				local function addRawMatch(index, scr, source)
					rawMatches[#rawMatches + 1] = {
						Index = index,
						Script = scr,
					}
					if snippetLimit > 0 and type(source) == "string" and not snippetMap[scr] and #rawMatches <= snippetLimit then
						snippetMap[scr] = getSourceSnippet(source, term)
					end
				end

				local function addFailureDetail(index, scr, reason)
					failureDetails[#failureDetails + 1] = {
						Index = index,
						Script = scr,
						Reason = trim(reason or "") ~= "" and tostring(reason) or "The local decompiler failed without an error message.",
					}
				end

				local function processScript(index, scr)
					local cacheEntry = scriptSourceCache[scr]
					local source = type(cacheEntry) == "table" and cacheEntry[cacheKey] or nil
					local cachedFailureReason = assistantState.GetDecompilerFailureReason and assistantState.GetDecompilerFailureReason(source)
					if cachedFailureReason then
						failedCount = failedCount + 1
						addFailureDetail(index, scr, cachedFailureReason)
						return
					end
					if not source then
						local result, decompileErr = (env.dexDecompileScriptInMode or env.dexDecompileScript)(scr, lookupMode)
						if type(result) == "string" and #result > 0 then
							source = env.repairPossibleMojibake and env.repairPossibleMojibake(result) or result
							local failureReason = assistantState.GetDecompilerFailureReason and assistantState.GetDecompilerFailureReason(source)
							if failureReason then
								failedCount = failedCount + 1
								addFailureDetail(index, scr, failureReason)
								return
							end
							if type(cacheEntry) ~= "table" then
								cacheEntry = {}
								scriptSourceCache[scr] = cacheEntry
							end
							cacheEntry[cacheKey] = source
							local loweredBucket = assistantState.ScriptSourceLowerCache[scr]
							if type(loweredBucket) == "table" then
								loweredBucket[cacheKey] = nil
							end
						else
							failedCount = failedCount + 1
							addFailureDetail(index, scr, decompileErr)
							return
						end
					end

					if source and getLoweredSource(scr, source):find(loweredNeedle, 1, true) then
						addRawMatch(index, scr, source)
					end
				end

				local localBatchSearchUrl = cacheKey == "local" and getDexLocalSearchUrl() or nil
				local localBatchSearchFailed = false

				local function flushLocalBatch(batch)
					if #batch == 0 then
						return true
					end

					local requestScripts = {}
					local pendingById = {}
					for i = 1,#batch do
						local pending = batch[i]
						local encodedBytecode, bytecodeErr = getEncodedScriptBytecode(pending.Script)
						if encodedBytecode then
							requestScripts[#requestScripts + 1] = {
								id = i,
								bytecode = encodedBytecode,
							}
							pendingById[i] = pending
						else
							failedCount = failedCount + 1
							addFailureDetail(pending.Index, pending.Script, bytecodeErr or "DEX could not read bytecode for this script.")
						end
					end

					if #requestScripts == 0 then
						return true
					end

					local body = jsonEncode({
						term = term,
						scripts = requestScripts,
						max_matches = maxMatches and math.max(0, maxMatches - #rawMatches) or nil,
					})
					if not body then
						return false
					end

					local success, response = pcall(env.request, {
						Url = localBatchSearchUrl,
						Method = "POST",
						Headers = {
							["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
						},
						Body = body,
					})
					if not success or type(response) ~= "table" then
						return false
					end

					local statusCode, responseBody = getSearchResponseStatusAndBody(response)
					if statusCode and (statusCode < 200 or statusCode >= 300) then
						return false
					end

					local decoded = jsonDecode(responseBody)
					if type(decoded) ~= "table" or type(decoded.matches) ~= "table" then
						return false
					end

					failedCount = failedCount + (tonumber(decoded.failed) or 0)
					if decoded.truncated then
						truncated = true
					end
					if type(decoded.failures) == "table" then
						for i = 1,#decoded.failures do
							local failure = decoded.failures[i]
							local pending = failure and pendingById[failure.id]
							if pending then
								addFailureDetail(pending.Index, pending.Script, failure.reason)
							end
						end
					end

					for i = 1,#decoded.matches do
						local pending = pendingById[decoded.matches[i]]
						if pending then
							local snippetSource = nil
							if snippetLimit > 0 and #rawMatches < snippetLimit then
								snippetSource = getScriptSource(pending.Script)
							end
							addRawMatch(pending.Index, pending.Script, snippetSource)
						end
					end

					return true
				end

				local batch = {}
				local batchSize = options.batchSize
				if not batchSize then
					if cacheKey == "local" then
						if #scripts >= 1500 then
							batchSize = 4
						elseif #scripts >= 600 then
							batchSize = 6
						elseif #scripts >= 200 then
							batchSize = 8
						else
							batchSize = 12
						end
					else
						batchSize = 96
					end
				end
				for index, scr in ipairs(scripts) do
					if env.getDexScriptSearchToken and env.getDexScriptSearchToken() ~= searchToken then
						truncated = true
						break
					end

					if maxScans and scannedCount >= maxScans then
						truncated = true
						break
					end

					scannedCount = scannedCount + 1

					local cacheEntry = scriptSourceCache[scr]
					local cachedSource = type(cacheEntry) == "table" and cacheEntry[cacheKey] or nil
					if cachedSource then
						if getLoweredSource(scr, cachedSource):find(loweredNeedle, 1, true) then
							addRawMatch(index, scr, cachedSource)
						end
						completedCount = completedCount + 1
					elseif localBatchSearchUrl and not localBatchSearchFailed then
						batch[#batch + 1] = {
							Index = index,
							Script = scr,
						}
						if #batch >= batchSize then
							if not flushLocalBatch(batch) then
								localBatchSearchFailed = true
								for i = 1,#batch do
									processScript(batch[i].Index, batch[i].Script)
								end
							end
							completedCount = completedCount + #batch
							reportProgress(true)
							table.clear(batch)
							task.wait()
						end
					else
						processScript(index, scr)
						completedCount = completedCount + 1
					end

					if maxMatches and #rawMatches >= maxMatches then
						truncated = index < #scripts
						break
					end

					if yieldEvery > 0 and index % yieldEvery == 0 then
						reportProgress(false)
						task.wait()
					end
				end

				if #batch > 0 then
					if not (localBatchSearchUrl and not localBatchSearchFailed and flushLocalBatch(batch)) then
						for i = 1,#batch do
							processScript(batch[i].Index, batch[i].Script)
						end
					end
					completedCount = completedCount + #batch
					reportProgress(true)
					table.clear(batch)
					task.wait()
				end

				reportProgress(true)

				if #rawMatches > 1 then
					table.sort(rawMatches, function(a, b)
						return a.Index < b.Index
					end)
				end
				if #failureDetails > 1 then
					table.sort(failureDetails, function(a, b)
						return (a.Index or 0) < (b.Index or 0)
					end)
				end
				local undisclosedFailureCount = math.max(0, failedCount - #failureDetails)

				if maxMatches and #rawMatches > maxMatches then
					truncated = true
					for i = maxMatches + 1,#rawMatches do
						rawMatches[i] = nil
					end
				end

				for i = 1,#rawMatches do
					matches[i] = rawMatches[i].Script
				end

				return matches, completedCount, failedCount, nil, truncated, #scripts, snippetMap, os.clock() - startedAt, skippedDefaultCount or 0, failureDetails, undisclosedFailureCount
			end

			local function extractNameTerms(message)
				local fallback = function(text)
					text = trim(text)
					text = text:gsub("^[Ff]ind%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ss]earch%s+for%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ss]earch%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ss]how%s+me%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ll]ocate%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ll]ook%s+for%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^an?%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^the%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^(gui|ui|script|part|instance|textbutton|textlabel|textbox|imagebutton|imagelabel|screengui|surfacegui|billboardgui|localscript|modulescript|remote|proximityprompt|proximity prompt)%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^with%s+name%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^named%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^called%s+", "")
					return text
				end

				return extractNeedles(message, {
					"[Nn]amed%s+(.+)",
					"[Cc]alled%s+(.+)",
					"[Ff]or%s+(.+)$"
				}, fallback)
			end

			local function extractTextTerms(message)
				local fallback = function(text)
					text = trim(text)
					text = text:gsub("^[Ff]ind%s+visible%s+text%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ff]ind%s+text%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ss]earch%s+text%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ss]earch%s+for%s+text%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ll]ook%s+for%s+text%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ff]ind%s+words?%s+", "")
					return text
				end

				return extractNeedles(message, {
					"[Tt]ext%s+(.+)$",
					"[Ww]ord%s+(.+)$",
					"[Ww]ords%s+(.+)$"
				}, fallback)
			end

			local function extractScriptTerms(message)
				local fallback = function(text)
					text = trim(text)
					text = text:gsub("^[Ff]ind%s+a%s+script%s+that%s+contains%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ff]ind%s+scripts%s+that%s+contain%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ss]earch%s+scripts%s+for%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ss]earch%s+script%s+source%s+for%s+", "")
					return text
				end

				return extractNeedles(message, {
					"[Cc]ontains?%s+the%s+word%s+(.+)",
					"[Cc]ontains?%s+the%s+text%s+(.+)",
					"[Cc]ontains?%s+(.+)",
					"[Ww]ith%s+the%s+word%s+(.+)",
					"[Ww]ith%s+the%s+text%s+(.+)",
					"[Ff]or%s+(.+)$"
				}, fallback)
			end

			local function extractNavigationTerms(message)
				local fallback = function(text)
					text = trim(text)
					text = text:gsub("^[Bb]ring%s+me%s+to%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Tt]ake%s+me%s+to%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Gg]o%s+to%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Jj]ump%s+to%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Oo]pen%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ss]elect%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^[Ss]how%s+me%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^an?%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^the%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^(gui|ui|script|part|instance|textbutton|textlabel|textbox|imagebutton|imagelabel|screengui|surfacegui|billboardgui|localscript|modulescript|remote|proximityprompt|proximity prompt)%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^with%s+name%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^named%s+", "")
					text = text:gsub("^called%s+", "")
					return text
				end

				return extractNeedles(message, {
					"[Bb]ring%s+me%s+to%s+(.+)$",
					"[Tt]ake%s+me%s+to%s+(.+)$",
					"[Gg]o%s+to%s+(.+)$",
					"[Jj]ump%s+to%s+(.+)$",
					"[Oo]pen%s+(.+)$",
					"[Ss]elect%s+(.+)$",
					"[Ss]how%s+me%s+(.+)$"
				}, fallback)
			end

			local function buildScriptSummary(scriptObj, source)
				local lineCount = select(2, source:gsub("\n", "\n")) + 1
				local services, serviceSeen = {}, {}
				local eventNames, eventSeen = {}, {}
				local functionNames, functionSeen = {}, {}
				local stringLiterals, stringSeen = {}, {}
				local categories = {
					{Label = "controls UI", Score = 0, Patterns = {"screengui", "textlabel", "textbutton", "textbox", "billboardgui", "surfacegui", ".visible", ".text", "playergui"}},
					{Label = "handles interaction or prompts", Score = 0, Patterns = {"proximityprompt", "clickdetector", "triggered", "actiontext", "objecttext", "prompt"}},
					{Label = "talks to remotes/client-server code", Score = 0, Patterns = {"fireserver", "invokeserver", "onclientevent", "onserverevent", "remoteevent", "remotefunction"}},
					{Label = "works with characters or humanoids", Score = 0, Patterns = {"humanoid", "characteradded", "humanoidrootpart", "localplayer"}},
					{Label = "moves or edits world objects", Score = 0, Patterns = {"cframe", "workspace", "basepart", "pivotto", "position", "orientation"}},
					{Label = "looks shop or inventory related", Score = 0, Patterns = {"sell", "store", "shop", "loot", "inventory", "price", "currency"}},
					{Label = "uses tweens or animation", Score = 0, Patterns = {"tweenservice", "tweeninfo", "animation", "animator"}},
					{Label = "stores or loads data", Score = 0, Patterns = {"datastoreservice", "getasync", "setasync", "updateasync"}},
					{Label = "plays or edits sounds", Score = 0, Patterns = {"soundservice", "sound", ":play(", "playbackspeed"}},
				}

				for match in source:gmatch('GetService%(%s*["' .. "'" .. ']([%w_]+)["' .. "'" .. ']%s*%)') do
					pushUnique(services, serviceSeen, match, 6)
				end
				for match in source:gmatch(":([%w_]+)Connect%s*%(") do
					pushUnique(eventNames, eventSeen, match, 6)
				end
				for match in source:gmatch("local%s+function%s+([%w_]+)%s*%(") do
					pushUnique(functionNames, functionSeen, match, 6)
				end
				for match in source:gmatch("function%s+([%w_%.:]+)%s*%(") do
					pushUnique(functionNames, functionSeen, match, 6)
				end
				for match in source:gmatch("([%w_]+)%s*=%s*function%s*%(") do
					pushUnique(functionNames, functionSeen, match, 6)
				end
				for match in source:gmatch('["' .. "'" .. ']([^"\n\r' .. "'" .. ']+)["' .. "'" .. ']') do
					match = trim(match)
					if #match >= 4 and #match <= 42 and match:match("[%a]") and not match:match("^rbxasset") and not match:match("^https?://") then
						pushUnique(stringLiterals, stringSeen, match, 6)
					end
				end

				local loweredSource = lower(source)
				for i = 1,#categories do
					local score = 0
					for p = 1,#categories[i].Patterns do
						if loweredSource:find(categories[i].Patterns[p], 1, true) then
							score = score + 1
						end
					end
					categories[i].Score = score
				end
				table.sort(categories, function(a, b) return a.Score > b.Score end)

				local topCategories = {}
				for i = 1,#categories do
					if categories[i].Score > 0 then
						topCategories[#topCategories + 1] = categories[i].Label
					end
					if #topCategories >= 3 then break end
				end

				local lines = {}
				lines[#lines + 1] = ("Script: [%s] %s"):format(scriptObj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(scriptObj))
				if #topCategories > 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "Summary: This looks like a script that " .. joinList(topCategories) .. "."
				else
					lines[#lines + 1] = "Summary: I could not pin it to one exact job, but I can still outline the main signals and helpers it uses."
				end
				lines[#lines + 1] = ("Size: about %d lines."):format(lineCount)
				if #services > 0 then lines[#lines + 1] = "Services: " .. table.concat(services, ", ") end
				if #eventNames > 0 then lines[#lines + 1] = "Events: " .. table.concat(eventNames, ", ") end
				if #functionNames > 0 then lines[#lines + 1] = "Functions: " .. table.concat(functionNames, ", ") end
				if #stringLiterals > 0 then lines[#lines + 1] = "Interesting text: " .. table.concat(stringLiterals, ", ") end
				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			local function buildSourceExcerpt(source, maxChars)
				maxChars = maxChars or 12000
				if #source <= maxChars then
					return source, false
				end

				local headSize = math.floor(maxChars * 0.45)
				local middleSize = math.floor(maxChars * 0.2)
				local tailSize = maxChars - headSize - middleSize
				local middleStart = math.max(headSize + 1, math.floor((#source - middleSize) / 2))
				local middleEnd = math.min(#source, middleStart + middleSize - 1)
				local tailStart = math.max(middleEnd + 1, #source - tailSize + 1)

				local excerpt = table.concat({
					source:sub(1, headSize),
					"\n-- [Dex trimmed this script and kept a middle excerpt to avoid overloading the AI] --\n",
					source:sub(middleStart, middleEnd),
					"\n-- [Dex also kept the tail of the script] --\n",
					source:sub(tailStart),
				})

				return excerpt, true
			end

			local function collectInterestingSourceLines(source, limit)
				local lines, seen = {}, {}
				local patterns = {
					"function",
					"connect",
					"fireserver",
					"invokeserver",
					"remote",
					"getservice",
					"require(",
					"waitforchild",
					"loadstring",
					"http",
					"webhook",
					"teleportservice",
					"datastore",
					"proximityprompt",
					"humanoid",
					"tweenservice",
				}

				for rawLine in source:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
					local line = trim(rawLine)
					if line ~= "" and #line <= 180 then
						local loweredLine = lower(line)
						for i = 1,#patterns do
							if loweredLine:find(patterns[i], 1, true) then
								pushUnique(lines, seen, truncateText(line, 180), limit)
								break
							end
						end
					end
					if limit and #lines >= limit then
						break
					end
				end

				return lines
			end

			local function buildSelectedObjectContext()
				local primary = getSelectedPrimaryNode()
				if not primary or not primary.Obj then
					return "Nothing is selected in Explorer right now."
				end

				local lines = {getSelectedSummary()}
				local propertyHits = getTextPropertyHits(primary.Obj, primary.Obj.Name)
				if #propertyHits > 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "Selected object text properties: " .. table.concat(propertyHits, ", ")
				end

				local childCount = math.min(#primary, 5)
				if childCount > 0 then
					local childLines = {}
					for i = 1,childCount do
						if primary[i] and primary[i].Obj then
							childLines[#childLines + 1] = ("- [%s] %s"):format(primary[i].Obj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(primary[i].Obj))
						end
					end
					if #childLines > 0 then
						lines[#lines + 1] = "Child preview:\n" .. table.concat(childLines, "\n")
					end
				end

				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			local function buildNamedSearchLocalText(term, kindLabel, matches)
				if #matches == 0 then
					return ('I could not find a %s named "%s".'):format(kindLabel, term)
				end

				return ('Found %d %s match%s for "%s". I selected the first one in Explorer.\n%s'):format(
					#matches,
					kindLabel,
					#matches == 1 and "" or "es",
					term,
					summarizeNodes(matches)
				)
			end

			assistantState.BuildSkippedScriptCountLine = function(skippedDefaultCount, richText)
				if type(skippedDefaultCount) ~= "number" or skippedDefaultCount <= 0 then
					return nil
				end

				local countText = richText and ("[[orange:%d]]"):format(skippedDefaultCount) or tostring(skippedDefaultCount)
				return ("Skipped Roblox/default scripts: %s"):format(countText)
			end

			local function buildScriptSearchLocalText(term, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount, richText)
				local lines = {}
				if err then
					lines[#lines + 1] = err
				elseif #matches == 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = ('I did not find any scripts containing "%s".'):format(term)
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Scanned: %d%s | Failed decompiles: %d"):format(
						scannedCount,
						totalScripts and totalScripts > 0 and ("/" .. totalScripts) or "",
						failedCount
					)
					local skippedLine = assistantState.BuildSkippedScriptCountLine and assistantState.BuildSkippedScriptCountLine(skippedDefaultCount, richText)
					if skippedLine then
						lines[#lines + 1] = skippedLine
					end
				else
					lines[#lines + 1] = ('Found %d script match%s containing "%s". I selected the first one in Explorer.'):format(
						#matches,
						#matches == 1 and "" or "es",
						term
					)
					lines[#lines + 1] = summarizeScriptObjects(matches)
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Scanned: %d%s | Failed decompiles: %d"):format(
						scannedCount,
						totalScripts and totalScripts > 0 and ("/" .. totalScripts) or "",
						failedCount
					)
					local skippedLine = assistantState.BuildSkippedScriptCountLine and assistantState.BuildSkippedScriptCountLine(skippedDefaultCount, richText)
					if skippedLine then
						lines[#lines + 1] = skippedLine
					end

					if truncated then
						lines[#lines + 1] = "The search was capped early to keep the assistant responsive."
					end
				end

				if type(elapsedSeconds) == "number" and elapsedSeconds > 0 then
					local formattedSeconds = elapsedSeconds >= 10 and ("%.1fs"):format(elapsedSeconds) or ("%.2fs"):format(elapsedSeconds)
					local lookupMode = assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto"
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Finished in %s using %s lookup."):format(formattedSeconds, lookupMode)
				end

				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			local function buildTextSearchLocalText(term, textMatches, scriptMatches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, focused, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount, richText)
				local lines = {}
				if #textMatches == 0 and #scriptMatches == 0 then
					if err then
						lines[#lines + 1] = ('I could not find "%s" in Explorer text properties, and script-source search is unavailable: %s'):format(term, err)
					else
						lines[#lines + 1] = ('I could not find "%s" in object text properties or script source.'):format(term)
					end
				else
					lines[#lines + 1] = ('Search results for "%s":'):format(term)
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Explorer text/property matches: %d"):format(#textMatches)
					if #textMatches > 0 then
						lines[#lines + 1] = summarizeTextResults(textMatches)
					end

					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Script source matches: %d"):format(#scriptMatches)
					if err == nil then
						lines[#lines + 1] = ("Script scan: %d%s scanned, %d failed decompiles"):format(
							scannedCount,
							totalScripts and totalScripts > 0 and ("/" .. totalScripts) or "",
							failedCount
						)
						local skippedLine = assistantState.BuildSkippedScriptCountLine and assistantState.BuildSkippedScriptCountLine(skippedDefaultCount, richText)
						if skippedLine then
							lines[#lines + 1] = skippedLine
						end
					end
					if #scriptMatches > 0 then
						lines[#lines + 1] = summarizeScriptObjects(scriptMatches)
					elseif err then
						lines[#lines + 1] = err
					end

					if truncated then
						lines[#lines + 1] = "The script-source search was capped early so the AI would not get overloaded."
					end

					if focused then
						lines[#lines + 1] = "I selected the first match in Explorer."
					end
				end

				if type(elapsedSeconds) == "number" and elapsedSeconds > 0 then
					local formattedSeconds = elapsedSeconds >= 10 and ("%.1fs"):format(elapsedSeconds) or ("%.2fs"):format(elapsedSeconds)
					local lookupMode = assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto"
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Finished in %s using %s lookup."):format(formattedSeconds, lookupMode)
				end

				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			local function isScriptKindLabel(kindLabel)
				return kindLabel == "script" or kindLabel == "Script" or kindLabel == "LocalScript" or kindLabel == "ModuleScript"
			end

			local function buildNavigationResultText(term, kindLabel, nameMatches, textMatches, scriptMatches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap)
				if #nameMatches == 0 and #textMatches == 0 and #scriptMatches == 0 then
					if err and (kindLabel == "instance" or isScriptKindLabel(kindLabel)) then
						return ('I could not bring you to "%s". Explorer name search found nothing, and script-source search is unavailable: %s'):format(term, err)
					end
					return ('I could not bring you to a %s matching "%s".'):format(kindLabel, term)
				end

				local lines = {}
				if #nameMatches > 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = ('I brought you to the first %s match for "%s".'):format(kindLabel, term)
					lines[#lines + 1] = summarizeNodes(nameMatches)
				elseif #textMatches > 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = ('I brought you to the first Explorer text match for "%s".'):format(term)
					lines[#lines + 1] = summarizeTextResults(textMatches)
				else
					lines[#lines + 1] = ('I brought you to the first script match for "%s".'):format(term)
					lines[#lines + 1] = summarizeScriptObjects(scriptMatches)
				end

				if #scriptMatches > 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Script scan: %d%s scanned, %d failed decompiles"):format(
						scannedCount,
						totalScripts and totalScripts > 0 and ("/" .. totalScripts) or "",
						failedCount
					)
					local snippetLines = {}
					for i = 1,math.min(#scriptMatches, 2) do
						local snippet = snippetMap and snippetMap[scriptMatches[i]]
						if snippet then
							snippetLines[#snippetLines + 1] = ("%d. %s"):format(i, snippet)
						end
					end
					if #snippetLines > 0 then
						lines[#lines + 1] = "Example matching code:\n" .. table.concat(snippetLines, "\n")
					end
				end

				if truncated then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "The script-source scan was capped early to keep the request light."
				end

				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			local function handleNavigationRequest(message, lowered, usingReal)
				local kindLabel, kindFunc = resolveKind(lowered)
				local terms = extractNavigationTerms(message)

				if #terms == 0 then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Tell me what to bring you to, like "bring me to remote shop", "go to script proximity", or "select gui sell loot".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				for i = 1,#terms do
					local term = terms[i]
					local nameMatches = searchByName(term, kindFunc, 25)
					local textMatches, scriptMatches = {}, {}
					local scannedCount, failedCount = 0, 0
					local err, truncated, totalScripts = nil, false, 0
					local snippetMap = {}
					local focused = false

					if usingReal then
						addThinkingMessage("Dex", ('Trying to bring Explorer to %s "%s".'):format(kindLabel, term), thinkingMessageColor)
					end

					if #nameMatches > 0 then
						focused = focusNode(nameMatches[1])
						if usingReal then
							addThinkingMessage("Dex", "Found a direct Explorer name match.", thinkingMessageColor)
						end
					else
						if (kindLabel == "instance" or kindLabel == "GUI") then
							if usingReal then
								addThinkingMessage("Dex", "No direct name match. Checking visible text properties.", thinkingMessageColor)
							end
							textMatches = searchByTextProperties(term, 12)
							if #textMatches > 0 then
								focused = focusNode(textMatches[1].Node)
							end
						end

						if not focused and (kindLabel == "instance" or isScriptKindLabel(kindLabel)) then
							if usingReal then
								addThinkingMessage("Dex", "Checking script source to find the target.", thinkingMessageColor)
							end
							scriptMatches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap = searchScriptsByText(term, {
								snippetLimit = 0,
								maxMatches = usingReal and 20 or nil,
								maxScans = usingReal and 160 or nil,
							})
							if #scriptMatches > 0 and nodes[scriptMatches[1]] then
								focused = focusNode(nodes[scriptMatches[1]])
							end
						end
					end

					if usingReal and focused then
						addThinkingMessage("Dex", "Focused the best match in Explorer.", thinkingMessageColor)
					end

					local resultEntries = {}
