					appendResultEntries(resultEntries, buildNodeResultEntries(nameMatches, "Explorer name match", 5), 10)
					if #resultEntries < 10 then
						appendResultEntries(resultEntries, buildTextResultEntries(textMatches, math.max(0, 10 - #resultEntries)), 10)
					end
					if #resultEntries < 10 then
						appendResultEntries(resultEntries, buildScriptResultEntries(scriptMatches, snippetMap, math.max(0, 10 - #resultEntries), false), 10)
					end
					setResultEntries(('Results for "%s"'):format(term), resultEntries)

					addMessage(usingReal and "Real ai" or "Dex", buildNavigationResultText(term, kindLabel, nameMatches, textMatches, scriptMatches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap), usingReal and realMessageColor or defaultMessageColor)
				end
			end

			local function collectChildNodes(node, kindFunc, maxCount)
				local results = {}
				if not node then return results end

				for i = 1,#node do
					local child = node[i]
					if not child.Del and child.Obj and not isDexObject(child.Obj) and kindFunc(child.Obj) then
						results[#results + 1] = child
						if maxCount and #results >= maxCount then
							break
						end
					end
				end

				return results
			end

			local function countChildNodes(node, kindFunc)
				local count = 0
				if not node then return count end

				for i = 1,#node do
					local child = node[i]
					if not child.Del and child.Obj and not isDexObject(child.Obj) and kindFunc(child.Obj) then
						count = count + 1
					end
				end

				return count
			end

			local function buildChildrenResultText(selectedNode, kindLabel, children, totalMatches)
				local itemLabel = kindLabel == "instance" and "children" or (kindLabel .. " children")
				if totalMatches == 0 then
					return ('The selected object does not have any %s I could show.'):format(itemLabel)
				end

				local lines = {
					('Selected: [%s] %s'):format(selectedNode.Obj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(selectedNode.Obj)),
					('Showing %d of %d %s:'):format(#children, totalMatches, itemLabel),
					summarizeNodes(children, 8),
				}
				if totalMatches > #children then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "There are more matches under this selection. Refine the request if you want a smaller list."
				end
				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			local function handleChildrenRequest(message, lowered, usingReal)
				local selectedNode = getSelectedPrimaryNode()
				if not selectedNode or not selectedNode.Obj then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Select something in Explorer first, then ask "show children" or "show remote children".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				local kindLabel, kindFunc = resolveKind(lowered)
				local children = collectChildNodes(selectedNode, kindFunc, 8)
				local totalMatches = countChildNodes(selectedNode, kindFunc)

				if usingReal then
					addThinkingMessage("Dex", ('Checking the selected object for %s.'):format(kindLabel == "instance" and "children" or (kindLabel .. " children")), thinkingMessageColor)
				end

				setResultEntries(
					kindLabel == "instance" and "Children" or ((kindLabel:gsub("^%l", string.upper)) .. " children"),
					buildNodeResultEntries(children, "child", 10)
				)
				addMessage(usingReal and "Real ai" or "Dex", buildChildrenResultText(selectedNode, kindLabel, children, totalMatches), usingReal and realMessageColor or defaultMessageColor)
			end

			local function handleParentRequest(usingReal)
				local selectedNode = getSelectedPrimaryNode()
				if not selectedNode or not selectedNode.Obj then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Select something in Explorer first, then ask me to go to its parent.', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				local parentObj = selectedNode.Obj.Parent
				local parentNode = parentObj and nodes[parentObj]
				if not parentObj or not parentNode or isDexObject(parentObj) then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage(usingReal and "Real ai" or "Dex", "I could not move to a parent for that selection.", usingReal and realMessageColor or defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				if usingReal then
					addThinkingMessage("Dex", "Moving Explorer selection to the parent object.", thinkingMessageColor)
				end

				focusNode(parentNode)
				setResultEntries("Parent", buildNodeResultEntries({parentNode}, "parent", 1))
				addMessage(usingReal and "Real ai" or "Dex", ("I brought you to the parent: [%s] %s"):format(parentObj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(parentObj)), usingReal and realMessageColor or defaultMessageColor)
			end

			local assistantAi = {}
			assistantAi.callRealAi = function()
				return nil, "Real ai is still initializing. Try again in a moment."
			end

			do
			local function buildRemoteKindSummary(remoteObj)
				if safeIsA(remoteObj, "RemoteFunction") then
					return "request/response calls between client and server."
				elseif safeIsA(remoteObj, "BindableFunction") then
					return "request/response style calls between scripts on the same side."
				elseif safeIsA(remoteObj, "BindableEvent") then
					return "fire-and-listen messages between scripts on the same side."
				elseif safeIsA(remoteObj, "UnreliableRemoteEvent") then
					return "fast fire-and-forget messages where some updates may be dropped."
				end
				return "fire-and-forget event messages between client and server."
			end

			local function searchScriptsForRemote(remoteObj, options)
				options = options or {}
				if not remoteObj then
					return {}, 0, 0, "No remote is selected.", false, 0, {}
				end
				return searchScriptsByText(remoteObj.Name, {
					snippetLimit = options.snippetLimit or 3,
					maxMatches = options.maxMatches,
					maxScans = options.maxScans or 160,
				})
			end

			local function buildRemoteEntries(remoteNode, matches, snippetMap, maxCount)
				local entries = {}
				if remoteNode then
					appendResultEntries(entries, buildNodeResultEntries({remoteNode}, "selected remote", 1), maxCount)
				end
				if maxCount == nil or #entries < maxCount then
					appendResultEntries(entries, buildScriptResultEntries(matches, snippetMap, maxCount and math.max(0, maxCount - #entries) or nil), maxCount)
				end
				return entries
			end

			local function buildSelectedRemoteLocalText(remoteNode, originNode, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap)
				local remoteObj = remoteNode.Obj
				local lines = {
					("Remote: [%s] %s"):format(remoteObj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(remoteObj)),
					("Summary: This remote likely handles %s"):format(buildRemoteKindSummary(remoteObj)),
				}

				if originNode and originNode ~= remoteNode then
					table.insert(lines, 1, ("I found a remote under your current selection and analyzed that one.\nParent selection: [%s] %s"):format(
						originNode.Obj.ClassName,
						Explorer.GetInstancePath(originNode.Obj)
					))
				end

				if err then
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Related script scan is unavailable: %s"):format(err)
					return table.concat(lines, "\n")
				end

				if #matches == 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("I did not find any decompiled scripts mentioning the remote name \"%s\"."):format(remoteObj.Name)
					return table.concat(lines, "\n")
				end

				lines[#lines + 1] = ("Scripts mentioning \"%s\": %d"):format(remoteObj.Name, #matches)
				lines[#lines + 1] = summarizeScriptObjects(matches)
				lines[#lines + 1] = ("Script scan: %d%s scanned, %d failed decompiles"):format(
					scannedCount,
					totalScripts and totalScripts > 0 and ("/" .. totalScripts) or "",
					failedCount
				)

				if truncated then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "The script scan was capped early to keep the request light."
				end

				local snippetLines = {}
				for i = 1,math.min(#matches, 3) do
					local snippet = snippetMap and snippetMap[matches[i]]
					if snippet then
						snippetLines[#snippetLines + 1] = ("%d. %s"):format(i, snippet)
					end
				end
				if #snippetLines > 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "Matching usage snippets:\n" .. table.concat(snippetLines, "\n")
				end

				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			local function buildSelectedRemoteAIContext(remoteNode, originNode, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap)
				local lines = {
					buildSelectedRemoteLocalText(remoteNode, originNode, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap),
					buildSelectedObjectContext(),
				}
				return table.concat(lines, "\n\n")
			end

			local function buildRemoteUsageResultText(remoteNode, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap)
				local remoteObj = remoteNode.Obj
				if err then
					return ("I could not search scripts for the selected remote \"%s\": %s"):format(remoteObj.Name, err)
				end
				if #matches == 0 then
					return ("I did not find any decompiled scripts mentioning the selected remote \"%s\"."):format(remoteObj.Name)
				end

				local lines = {
					('Found %d script match%s for the selected remote "%s". I selected the first one in Explorer.'):format(
						#matches,
						#matches == 1 and "" or "es",
						remoteObj.Name
					),
					summarizeScriptObjects(matches),
					("Script scan: %d%s scanned, %d failed decompiles"):format(
						scannedCount,
						totalScripts and totalScripts > 0 and ("/" .. totalScripts) or "",
						failedCount
					),
				}

				if truncated then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "The script scan was capped early to avoid overloading the search."
				end

				local snippetLines = {}
				for i = 1,math.min(#matches, 3) do
					local snippet = snippetMap and snippetMap[matches[i]]
					if snippet then
						snippetLines[#snippetLines + 1] = ("%d. %s"):format(i, snippet)
					end
				end
				if #snippetLines > 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "Matching usage snippets:\n" .. table.concat(snippetLines, "\n")
				end

				return table.concat(lines, "\n")
			end

			local function explainSelectedRemoteLocal()
				local remoteNode, originNode = getSelectedRemoteNode()
				if not remoteNode then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Highlight a RemoteEvent, RemoteFunction, UnreliableRemoteEvent, BindableEvent, or BindableFunction first, then ask "explain selected remote".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				local matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap = searchScriptsForRemote(remoteNode.Obj, {
					snippetLimit = 3,
					maxMatches = 12,
				})

				if originNode and originNode ~= remoteNode then
					focusNode(remoteNode)
				end

				setResultEntries(("Remote: %s"):format(remoteNode.Obj.Name), buildRemoteEntries(remoteNode, matches, snippetMap, 10))
				addMessage("Dex", buildSelectedRemoteLocalText(remoteNode, originNode, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap), defaultMessageColor)
			end

			local function explainSelectedRemoteReal(userMessage)
				local remoteNode, originNode = getSelectedRemoteNode()
				if not remoteNode then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Highlight a RemoteEvent, RemoteFunction, UnreliableRemoteEvent, BindableEvent, or BindableFunction first, then ask "explain selected remote".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				local matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap = searchScriptsForRemote(remoteNode.Obj, {
					snippetLimit = 3,
					maxMatches = 12,
				})

				if originNode and originNode ~= remoteNode then
					focusNode(remoteNode)
				end

				setResultEntries(("Remote: %s"):format(remoteNode.Obj.Name), buildRemoteEntries(remoteNode, matches, snippetMap, 10))
				addThinkingMessage("Dex", "Prepared remote details and matching script snippets for Real ai.", thinkingMessageColor)

				local reply, aiErr = assistantAi.callRealAi(
					userMessage,
					"Explain what the selected Roblox remote likely does. Mention whether it is an event or function, what kind of client/server traffic it likely handles, and what the matching scripts suggest about how it is used.",
					buildSelectedRemoteAIContext(remoteNode, originNode, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap),
					520,
					{RequestLabel = "Asking Real ai to explain the selected remote"}
				)

				if reply then
					addMessage("Real ai", reply, realMessageColor)
				else
					addMessage("Dex", "Real ai failed on that remote, so I used Dex ai instead.\n" .. tostring(aiErr), defaultMessageColor)
					addMessage("Dex", buildSelectedRemoteLocalText(remoteNode, originNode, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap), defaultMessageColor)
				end
			end

			local function handleSelectedRemoteUsageRequest(usingReal)
				local remoteNode = getSelectedRemoteNode()
				if not remoteNode then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Select a remote in Explorer first, then ask "show scripts that use it" or "bring me to the script that fires it".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				if usingReal then
					addThinkingMessage("Dex", "Looking for scripts that reference the selected remote.", thinkingMessageColor)
				end

				local matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap = searchScriptsForRemote(remoteNode.Obj, {
					snippetLimit = 3,
					maxMatches = usingReal and 20 or 12,
				})
				if #matches > 0 and nodes[matches[1]] then
					focusNode(nodes[matches[1]])
				end

				setResultEntries(("Remote usage: %s"):format(remoteNode.Obj.Name), buildRemoteEntries(remoteNode, matches, snippetMap, 10))
				addMessage(usingReal and "Real ai" or "Dex", buildRemoteUsageResultText(remoteNode, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap), usingReal and realMessageColor or defaultMessageColor)
			end

			assistantState.OpenConnectedScriptsSearch = function(node)
				if not node or not node.Obj then
					return
				end

				if requestBusy then
					window:Show()
					addMessage("Dex", "Explorer AI is already busy. Wait for the current task to finish first.", defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				window:Show()
				setResultEntries(("Connected scripts: %s"):format(node.Obj.Name), {})
				addMessage("Dex", ("Looking for scripts connected to [%s] %s ..."):format(node.Obj.ClassName, Explorer.GetInstancePath(node.Obj)), defaultMessageColor)
				setBusy(true)

				task.spawn(function()
					local progressLabel = addMessage("Dex", "0/0", defaultMessageColor)
					local packed = table.pack(pcall(function()
						return assistantState.SearchScriptsConnectedToNode(node, {
							progress = function(doneCount, totalCount)
								if progressLabel and progressLabel.Parent then
									progressLabel.Text = ("Dex: %d/%d"):format(doneCount, totalCount)
								end
							end,
						})
					end))

					setBusy(false)
					if not packed[1] then
						setResultEntries(("Connected scripts: %s"):format(node.Obj.Name), {})
						addMessage("Dex", "Connected-script scan failed unexpectedly.\n" .. tostring(packed[2]), defaultMessageColor)
						return
					end

					local candidates = packed[2]
					local err = packed[3]
					local terms = packed[4] or {}
					local scannedCount = packed[5]
					local failedCount = packed[6]
					local totalScripts = packed[7]
					local skippedDefaultCount = packed[8]
					local failureDetails = packed[9]
					local elapsedSeconds = packed[10]
					local truncated = packed[11]
					local undisclosedFailureCount = packed[12]

					setResultEntries(("Connected scripts: %s"):format(node.Obj.Name), assistantState.BuildConnectedScriptEntries(candidates, 20))
					addMessage("Dex", assistantState.BuildConnectedScriptsLocalText(node, candidates, terms, scannedCount, failedCount, err, totalScripts, skippedDefaultCount, elapsedSeconds, truncated), defaultMessageColor)
					assistantState.AddDecompilerFailureReportButton(("Failed decompiles while scanning connected scripts for %s"):format(node.Obj.Name), failedCount or 0, failureDetails, undisclosedFailureCount)
				end)
			end
			Explorer.AssistantBridge.OpenConnectedScripts = assistantState.OpenConnectedScriptsSearch

			assistantAi.buildSelectedScriptLocalText = function(scriptNode, source, originNode)
				local summary = buildScriptSummary(scriptNode.Obj, source)
				if originNode and originNode ~= scriptNode then
					return ("I found a script under your current selection and analyzed that one.\nParent selection: [%s] %s\n\n%s"):format(
						originNode.Obj.ClassName,
						Explorer.GetInstancePath(originNode.Obj),
						summary
					)
				end
				return summary
			end

			assistantAi.buildSelectedScriptAIContext = function(scriptNode, source, originNode)
				local lines = {assistantAi.buildSelectedScriptLocalText(scriptNode, source, originNode)}
				local interestingLines = collectInterestingSourceLines(source, 18)
				if #interestingLines > 0 then
					lines[#lines + 1] = "Highlighted code lines:\n" .. table.concat(interestingLines, "\n")
				end

				local excerpt, trimmed = buildSourceExcerpt(source, 12000)
				if trimmed then
					lines[#lines + 1] = ("Dex trimmed the script from %d characters to %d characters before sending it to the AI."):format(#source, #excerpt)
				end
				lines[#lines + 1] = "Source excerpt:\n" .. excerpt
				return table.concat(lines, "\n\n")
			end

			local function buildRealAiPrompt(userMessage, taskInstructions, context)
				return table.concat({
					"You are Dex Explorer AI inside a Roblox explorer/debugger.",
					"Only use the context Dex gives you.",
					"Never invent search hits, object names, paths, or script behavior that is not supported by the context.",
					"If Dex says the script or search was trimmed, say your answer is based on a partial view.",
					taskInstructions,
					"",
					"User request:",
					userMessage,
					"",
					"Dex context:",
					context,
				}, "\n")
			end

			local function getResponseStatusAndBody(response)
				local statusCode = tonumber(response and (response.StatusCode or response.Status or response.status_code or response.status))
				local responseBody = response and (response.Body or response.body or response.ResponseBody or response.responseBody)
				if type(response) == "string" and type(responseBody) ~= "string" then
					responseBody = response
				end
				return statusCode, responseBody
			end

			local function getDecodedErrorMessage(decoded)
				if type(decoded) ~= "table" then
					return nil
				end
				local err = decoded.error
				if type(err) == "table" then
					return err.message or err.status or err.code or err.type
				end
				if type(decoded.message) == "string" then
					return decoded.message
				end
				return nil
			end

			local function extractContentText(content)
				if type(content) == "string" then
					return trim(content)
				end
				if type(content) ~= "table" then
					return nil
				end

				local parts = {}
				for i = 1,#content do
					local part = content[i]
					if type(part) == "string" and part ~= "" then
						parts[#parts + 1] = part
					elseif type(part) == "table" then
						if type(part.text) == "string" and part.text ~= "" then
							parts[#parts + 1] = part.text
						elseif type(part.content) == "string" and part.content ~= "" then
							parts[#parts + 1] = part.content
						end
					end
				end

				return #parts > 0 and table.concat(parts, "\n") or nil
			end

			local function extractGeminiContent(decoded)
				if type(decoded) ~= "table" then return nil end
				local candidates = decoded.candidates
				if type(candidates) ~= "table" then return nil end
				local first = candidates[1]
				if type(first) ~= "table" then return nil end
				local contentData = first.content
				if type(contentData) ~= "table" then return nil end
				local parts = contentData.parts
				if type(parts) ~= "table" then return nil end

				local answerParts, thoughtParts = {}, {}
				for i = 1,#parts do
					if type(parts[i]) == "table" and type(parts[i].text) == "string" and parts[i].text ~= "" then
						if parts[i].thought then
							thoughtParts[#thoughtParts + 1] = parts[i].text
						else
							answerParts[#answerParts + 1] = parts[i].text
						end
					end
				end

				return (#answerParts > 0 and table.concat(answerParts, "\n") or nil), thoughtParts
			end

			local function extractOpenAiContent(decoded)
				if type(decoded) ~= "table" then
					return nil, {}
				end

				local thoughtParts = {}
				local answerText = type(decoded.output_text) == "string" and trim(decoded.output_text) or nil
				local outputItems = decoded.output
				if type(outputItems) == "table" then
					local answerParts = {}
					for i = 1,#outputItems do
						local item = outputItems[i]
						if type(item) == "table" then
							if item.type == "message" then
								local text = extractContentText(item.content)
								if text and text ~= "" then
									answerParts[#answerParts + 1] = text
								end
							elseif item.type == "reasoning" and type(item.summary) == "table" then
								for j = 1,#item.summary do
									local summary = item.summary[j]
									if type(summary) == "table" and type(summary.text) == "string" and summary.text ~= "" then
										thoughtParts[#thoughtParts + 1] = summary.text
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
					if (not answerText or answerText == "") and #answerParts > 0 then
						answerText = table.concat(answerParts, "\n")
					end
				end

				if #thoughtParts == 0 and type(decoded.reasoning) == "table" and type(decoded.reasoning.summary) == "table" then
					for i = 1,#decoded.reasoning.summary do
						local summary = decoded.reasoning.summary[i]
						if type(summary) == "table" and type(summary.text) == "string" and summary.text ~= "" then
							thoughtParts[#thoughtParts + 1] = summary.text
						end
					end
				end

				return answerText and trim(answerText) or nil, thoughtParts
			end

			local function extractChatCompletionContent(decoded)
				if type(decoded) ~= "table" then
					return nil, {}
				end

				local choices = decoded.choices
				if type(choices) ~= "table" or type(choices[1]) ~= "table" then
					return nil, {}
				end

				local messageData = choices[1].message
				if type(messageData) ~= "table" then
					return nil, {}
				end

				local text = extractContentText(messageData.content)
				return text and trim(text) or nil, {}
			end

			local function callGeminiRequest(prompt, modelMeta, apiKey, maxOutputTokens, canRequestThoughts)
				local payload = {
					contents = {
						{
							role = "user",
							parts = {
								{
									text = prompt
								}
							}
						}
					},
					generationConfig = {
						temperature = 0.2,
						maxOutputTokens = maxOutputTokens or 550,
					}
				}
				if canRequestThoughts then
					payload.generationConfig.thinkingConfig = {
						includeThoughts = true,
					}
					if modelMeta.ThinkingMode == "level" then
						payload.generationConfig.thinkingConfig.thinkingLevel = "low"
					else
						payload.generationConfig.thinkingConfig.thinkingBudget = -1
					end
				end

				local body = jsonEncode(payload)
				if not body then
					return nil, "DEX could not encode the Gemini request."
				end

				local url = ("https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/%s:generateContent?key=%s"):format(modelMeta.Code, apiKey)
				local success, response = pcall(env.request, {
					Url = url,
					Method = "POST",
					Headers = {
						["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
						},
						Body = body,
						Timeout = 25,
						timeout = 25,
					})
				if not success then
					return nil, tostring(response), {}, "Gemini"
				end

				local statusCode, responseBody = getResponseStatusAndBody(response)
				if type(responseBody) ~= "string" or responseBody == "" then
					return nil, "Gemini returned an empty response.", {}, "Gemini"
				end

				local decoded = jsonDecode(responseBody)
				if statusCode and (statusCode < 200 or statusCode >= 300) then
					return nil, getDecodedErrorMessage(decoded) or ("Gemini returned HTTP %d."):format(statusCode), {}, "Gemini"
				end

				local text, thoughts = extractGeminiContent(decoded)
				if text and trim(text) ~= "" then
					return trim(text), nil, thoughts or {}, "Gemini"
				end

				return nil, getDecodedErrorMessage(decoded) or "Gemini did not return any text.", thoughts or {}, "Gemini"
			end

			local function callOpenAiRequest(prompt, modelMeta, apiKey, maxOutputTokens, canRequestThoughts)
				local payload = {
					model = modelMeta.Code,
					input = prompt,
					max_output_tokens = maxOutputTokens or 550,
				}
				if canRequestThoughts then
					payload.reasoning = {
						effort = "low",
						summary = "auto",
					}
				end

				local body = jsonEncode(payload)
				if not body then
					return nil, "DEX could not encode the OpenAI request.", {}, "OpenAI"
				end

				local success, response = pcall(env.request, {
					Url = "https://api.openai.com/v1/responses",
					Method = "POST",
					Headers = {
						["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
						["Authorization"] = "Bearer " .. apiKey,
					},
					Body = body,
				})
				if not success then
					return nil, tostring(response), {}, "OpenAI"
				end

				local statusCode, responseBody = getResponseStatusAndBody(response)
				if type(responseBody) ~= "string" or responseBody == "" then
					return nil, "OpenAI returned an empty response.", {}, "OpenAI"
				end

				local decoded = jsonDecode(responseBody)
				if statusCode and (statusCode < 200 or statusCode >= 300) then
					return nil, getDecodedErrorMessage(decoded) or ("OpenAI returned HTTP %d."):format(statusCode), {}, "OpenAI"
				end

				local text, thoughts = extractOpenAiContent(decoded)
				if text and text ~= "" then
					return text, nil, thoughts or {}, "OpenAI"
				end

				return nil, getDecodedErrorMessage(decoded) or "OpenAI did not return any text.", thoughts or {}, "OpenAI"
			end

			local function callCompatibleChatRequest(prompt, modelMeta, apiKey, maxOutputTokens, providerName, requestUrl)
				local payload = {
					model = modelMeta.Code,
					messages = {
						{
							role = "user",
							content = prompt,
						}
					},
					temperature = 0.2,
					max_tokens = maxOutputTokens or 550,
				}

				local body = jsonEncode(payload)
				if not body then
					return nil, ("DEX could not encode the %s request."):format(providerName), {}, providerName
				end

				local success, response = pcall(env.request, {
					Url = requestUrl,
					Method = "POST",
					Headers = {
						["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
						["Authorization"] = "Bearer " .. apiKey,
					},
					Body = body,
				})
				if not success then
					return nil, tostring(response), {}, providerName
				end

				local statusCode, responseBody = getResponseStatusAndBody(response)
				if type(responseBody) ~= "string" or responseBody == "" then
					return nil, ("%s returned an empty response."):format(providerName), {}, providerName
				end

				local decoded = jsonDecode(responseBody)
				if statusCode and (statusCode < 200 or statusCode >= 300) then
					return nil, getDecodedErrorMessage(decoded) or ("%s returned HTTP %d."):format(providerName, statusCode), {}, providerName
				end

				local text, thoughts = extractChatCompletionContent(decoded)
				if text and text ~= "" then
					return text, nil, thoughts or {}, providerName
				end

				return nil, getDecodedErrorMessage(decoded) or ("%s did not return any text."):format(providerName), thoughts or {}, providerName
			end

			local function callRealAi(userMessage, taskInstructions, context, maxOutputTokens, options)
				options = options or {}
				syncApiKeyFromInput()
				local providerMeta = getCurrentProviderMeta()
				local modelMeta = getCurrentModelMeta()
				local apiKey = getCurrentApiKey()
				local wantsThinking = assistantState.ShowThinking
				local canRequestThoughts = wantsThinking and modelMeta.SupportsThinking
				if not env.request then
					return nil, "Your executor does not expose a request/http function for Real ai."
				end
				if apiKey == "" then
					return nil, ("Paste a %s API key first."):format(providerMeta.Name)
				end

				local prompt = buildRealAiPrompt(userMessage, taskInstructions, context)
				if wantsThinking then
					addThinkingMessage("Dex", ("%s with %s / %s."):format(options.RequestLabel or "Sending Real ai request", providerMeta.Name, modelMeta.Name), thinkingMessageColor)
					if not modelMeta.SupportsThinking then
						addThinkingMessage("Dex", unsupportedThinkingText, thinkingMessageColor)
					end
				end

				local text, err, thoughts, providerLabel
				if providerMeta.Code == "gemini" then
					text, err, thoughts, providerLabel = callGeminiRequest(prompt, modelMeta, apiKey, maxOutputTokens, canRequestThoughts)
				elseif providerMeta.Code == "openai" then
					text, err, thoughts, providerLabel = callOpenAiRequest(prompt, modelMeta, apiKey, maxOutputTokens, canRequestThoughts)
				elseif providerMeta.ChatUrl then
					text, err, thoughts, providerLabel = callCompatibleChatRequest(prompt, modelMeta, apiKey, maxOutputTokens, providerMeta.Name, providerMeta.ChatUrl)
				else
					return nil, ("DEX does not know how to call the %s provider yet."):format(providerMeta.Name)
				end

				if canRequestThoughts then
					if type(thoughts) == "table" and #thoughts > 0 then
						for i = 1,#thoughts do
							addThinkingMessage(providerLabel or providerMeta.Name, thoughts[i], Color3.fromRGB(58, 78, 102))
						end
					else
						addThinkingMessage("Dex", ("%s returned an answer without a thought summary for that request."):format(providerLabel or providerMeta.Name), thinkingMessageColor)
					end
				end

				return text, err
			end

			assistantAi.explainSelectedScriptLocal = function()
				local scriptNode, originNode = getSelectedScriptNode()
				if not scriptNode then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Highlight a Script, LocalScript, or ModuleScript in Explorer first, then ask "what does this script do?"', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				local source, err = getScriptSource(scriptNode.Obj)
				if not source then
					addMessage("Dex", err, defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				if originNode and originNode ~= scriptNode then
					focusNode(scriptNode)
				end

				setResultEntries(("Script: %s"):format(scriptNode.Obj.Name), buildNodeResultEntries({scriptNode}, "selected script", 1))
				addMessage("Dex", assistantAi.buildSelectedScriptLocalText(scriptNode, source, originNode), defaultMessageColor)
			end

			assistantAi.explainSelectedScriptReal = function(userMessage)
				local scriptNode, originNode = getSelectedScriptNode()
				if not scriptNode then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Highlight a Script, LocalScript, or ModuleScript in Explorer first, then ask "what does this script do?"', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				local source, err = getScriptSource(scriptNode.Obj)
				if not source then
					addMessage("Dex", err, defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				if originNode and originNode ~= scriptNode then
					focusNode(scriptNode)
				end
				setResultEntries(("Script: %s"):format(scriptNode.Obj.Name), buildNodeResultEntries({scriptNode}, "selected script", 1))
				addThinkingMessage("Dex", "Decompiled the selected script and prepared a trimmed excerpt for Real ai.", thinkingMessageColor)

				local reply, aiErr = assistantAi.callRealAi(
					userMessage,
					"Explain what the selected Roblox script does. Keep it concise but useful. Mention the main purpose, the key services/events/remotes it uses, and any suspicious behavior if present.",
					assistantAi.buildSelectedScriptAIContext(scriptNode, source, originNode),
					650,
					{RequestLabel = "Asking Real ai to explain the selected script"}
				)

				if reply then
					addMessage("Real ai", reply, realMessageColor)
				else
					addMessage("Dex", "Real ai failed on that script, so I used Dex ai instead.\n" .. tostring(aiErr), defaultMessageColor)
					addMessage("Dex", assistantAi.buildSelectedScriptLocalText(scriptNode, source, originNode), defaultMessageColor)
				end
			end

			assistantAi.callRealAi = callRealAi
			assistantAi.explainSelectedRemoteLocal = explainSelectedRemoteLocal
			assistantAi.explainSelectedRemoteReal = explainSelectedRemoteReal
			assistantAi.handleSelectedRemoteUsageRequest = handleSelectedRemoteUsageRequest
			end

			do
			assistantAi.handleNamedSearchLocal = function(message, lowered)
				local kindLabel, kindFunc = resolveKind(lowered)
				local terms = extractNameTerms(message)

				if #terms == 0 then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Tell me what name to look for, like "find a gui named sell loot" or "find a part named door".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				for i = 1,#terms do
					local term = terms[i]
					local matches = searchByName(term, kindFunc, 25)
					if #matches > 0 then
						focusNode(matches[1])
					end
					setResultEntries(('Results for "%s"'):format(term), buildNodeResultEntries(matches, "Explorer name match", 10))
					addMessage("Dex", buildNamedSearchLocalText(term, kindLabel, matches), defaultMessageColor)
				end
			end

			assistantAi.handleNamedSearchReal = function(message, lowered)
				local kindLabel, kindFunc = resolveKind(lowered)
				local terms = extractNameTerms(message)

				if #terms == 0 then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Tell me what name to look for, like "find a gui named sell loot" or "find a part named door".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				for i = 1,#terms do
					local term = terms[i]
					local matches = searchByName(term, kindFunc, 25)
					local textMatches, scriptMatches = {}, {}
					local scannedCount, failedCount = 0, 0
					local searchErr, truncated, totalScripts = nil, false, 0
					local snippetMap = {}
					local elapsedSeconds
					local focused = false
					addThinkingMessage("Dex", ('Searching Explorer names for %s "%s".'):format(kindLabel, term), thinkingMessageColor)

					if #matches > 0 then
						focused = focusNode(matches[1])
					elseif kindLabel == "instance" then
						addThinkingMessage("Dex", "No direct name match. Running broader text and script checks.", thinkingMessageColor)
						textMatches = searchByTextProperties(term, 12)
						scriptMatches, scannedCount, failedCount, searchErr, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds = searchScriptsByText(term, {
							snippetLimit = 0,
							maxMatches = 12,
							maxScans = 160,
						})

						if #textMatches > 0 then
							focused = focusNode(textMatches[1].Node)
						elseif #scriptMatches > 0 and nodes[scriptMatches[1]] then
							focused = focusNode(nodes[scriptMatches[1]])
						end
					end

					local contextLines = {
						buildNamedSearchLocalText(term, kindLabel, matches)
					}
					local resultEntries = {}
					appendResultEntries(resultEntries, buildNodeResultEntries(matches, "Explorer name match", 6), 10)
					if kindLabel == "instance" and (#textMatches > 0 or #scriptMatches > 0) then
						appendResultEntries(resultEntries, buildTextResultEntries(textMatches, math.max(0, 10 - #resultEntries)), 10)
						if #resultEntries < 10 then
							appendResultEntries(resultEntries, buildScriptResultEntries(scriptMatches, snippetMap, math.max(0, 10 - #resultEntries), false), 10)
						end
						contextLines[#contextLines + 1] = "Extra broad Explorer search:"
						contextLines[#contextLines + 1] = buildTextSearchLocalText(term, textMatches, scriptMatches, scannedCount, failedCount, searchErr, focused and #matches == 0, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds)
					end
					setResultEntries(('Results for "%s"'):format(term), resultEntries)
					local context = table.concat(contextLines, "\n\n")
					local reply, err = assistantAi.callRealAi(
						message,
						"Explain the Explorer search results in plain language. Mention the best match first. If Dex included a broader text/script search, use that too. If nothing was found, say that clearly. Keep the answer short.",
						context,
						400,
						{RequestLabel = "Asking Real ai to summarize the Explorer search"}
					)

					if reply then
						addMessage("Real ai", reply, realMessageColor)
						if type(elapsedSeconds) == "number" and elapsedSeconds > 0 then
							local formattedSeconds = elapsedSeconds >= 10 and ("%.1fs"):format(elapsedSeconds) or ("%.2fs"):format(elapsedSeconds)
							local lookupMode = assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto"
							addMessage("Dex", ("Finished in %s using %s lookup."):format(formattedSeconds, lookupMode), defaultMessageColor)
						end
					else
						addMessage("Dex", "Real ai failed on that search, so I used Dex ai instead.\n" .. tostring(err), defaultMessageColor)
						addMessage("Dex", context, defaultMessageColor)
					end
				end
			end

			assistantAi.handleScriptContentSearchLocal = function(message)
				local terms = extractScriptTerms(message)

				if #terms == 0 then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Tell me what script text to search for, like "find a script that contains the word proximity".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				for i = 1,#terms do
					local term = terms[i]
					local progressLabel = addMessage("Dex", "0/0", defaultMessageColor)
					local matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount, failureDetails, undisclosedFailureCount = searchScriptsByText(term, {
						snippetLimit = 0,
						progress = function(doneCount, totalCount)
							if progressLabel and progressLabel.Parent then
								progressLabel.Text = ("Dex: %d/%d"):format(doneCount, totalCount)
							end
						end,
					})
					if #matches > 0 and nodes[matches[1]] then
						focusNode(nodes[matches[1]])
					end
					setResultEntries(('Script results for "%s"'):format(term), buildScriptResultEntries(matches, snippetMap, 10, false))
					addMessage("Dex", buildScriptSearchLocalText(term, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount, true), defaultMessageColor, true)
					if failedCount > 0 then
						assistantState.AddChatActionButton(("Open debug report (%d failed)"):format(failedCount), function()
							local lines = {}
							if type(failureDetails) == "table" then
								for failureIndex = 1,#failureDetails do
									local failure = failureDetails[failureIndex]
									local scr = failure and failure.Script
									lines[#lines + 1] = ("%d. [%s] %s"):format(
										failureIndex,
										scr and scr.ClassName or "Script",
										scr and Explorer.GetInstancePath(scr) or "<unknown script>"
									)
									lines[#lines + 1] = ("   Reason: %s"):format(tostring(failure and failure.Reason or "The local decompiler failed without an error message."))
									lines[#lines + 1] = ""
								end
							end
							if (undisclosedFailureCount or 0) > 0 then
								lines[#lines + 1] = ("Plus %d more failed decompile(s) that did not include per-script detail."):format(undisclosedFailureCount)
							end
							if #lines == 0 then
								lines[1] = "DEX counted failed decompiles here, but did not receive per-script detail for them."
							end
							if assistantState.ShowDebugReport then
								assistantState.ShowDebugReport(('Failed decompiles for "%s"'):format(term), table.concat(lines, "\n"))
							end
						end, Color3.fromRGB(84, 62, 46))
					end
				end
			end

			assistantAi.handleScriptContentSearchReal = function(message)
				local terms = extractScriptTerms(message)

				if #terms == 0 then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Tell me what script text to search for, like "find a script that contains the word proximity".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				for i = 1,#terms do
					local term = terms[i]
					addThinkingMessage("Dex", ('Scanning script source for "%s" with capped results.'):format(term), thinkingMessageColor)
					local progressLabel = addMessage("Dex", "0/0", defaultMessageColor)
					local matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount, failureDetails, undisclosedFailureCount = searchScriptsByText(term, {
						snippetLimit = 0,
						maxMatches = 25,
						maxScans = 180,
						progress = function(doneCount, totalCount)
							if progressLabel and progressLabel.Parent then
								progressLabel.Text = ("Dex: %d/%d"):format(doneCount, totalCount)
							end
						end,
					})
					if #matches > 0 and nodes[matches[1]] then
						focusNode(nodes[matches[1]])
					end
					setResultEntries(('Script results for "%s"'):format(term), buildScriptResultEntries(matches, snippetMap, 10, false))

					local context = buildScriptSearchLocalText(term, matches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount)
					local reply, aiErr = assistantAi.callRealAi(
						message,
						"Summarize the script-source search results from Dex. Mention whether Dex found anything, which result looks best, and tell the player if the search was trimmed to avoid overload.",
						context,
						420,
						{RequestLabel = "Asking Real ai to explain the script-source search"}
					)

					if reply then
						addMessage("Real ai", reply, realMessageColor)
						if type(elapsedSeconds) == "number" and elapsedSeconds > 0 then
							local formattedSeconds = elapsedSeconds >= 10 and ("%.1fs"):format(elapsedSeconds) or ("%.2fs"):format(elapsedSeconds)
							local lookupMode = assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto"
							addMessage("Dex", ("Finished in %s using %s lookup."):format(formattedSeconds, lookupMode), defaultMessageColor)
						end
					else
						addMessage("Dex", "Real ai failed on that script search, so I used Dex ai instead.\n" .. tostring(aiErr), defaultMessageColor)
						addMessage("Dex", context, defaultMessageColor)
					end
					if failedCount > 0 then
						assistantState.AddChatActionButton(("Open debug report (%d failed)"):format(failedCount), function()
							local lines = {}
							if type(failureDetails) == "table" then
								for failureIndex = 1,#failureDetails do
									local failure = failureDetails[failureIndex]
									local scr = failure and failure.Script
									lines[#lines + 1] = ("%d. [%s] %s"):format(
										failureIndex,
										scr and scr.ClassName or "Script",
										scr and Explorer.GetInstancePath(scr) or "<unknown script>"
									)
									lines[#lines + 1] = ("   Reason: %s"):format(tostring(failure and failure.Reason or "The local decompiler failed without an error message."))
									lines[#lines + 1] = ""
								end
							end
							if (undisclosedFailureCount or 0) > 0 then
								lines[#lines + 1] = ("Plus %d more failed decompile(s) that did not include per-script detail."):format(undisclosedFailureCount)
							end
							if #lines == 0 then
								lines[1] = "DEX counted failed decompiles here, but did not receive per-script detail for them."
							end
							if assistantState.ShowDebugReport then
								assistantState.ShowDebugReport(('Failed decompiles for "%s"'):format(term), table.concat(lines, "\n"))
							end
						end, Color3.fromRGB(84, 62, 46))
					end
				end
			end

			assistantAi.handleTextSearchLocal = function(message)
				local terms = extractTextTerms(message)

				if #terms == 0 then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Tell me the text to look for, like "find text sell loot" or "find text general store".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				for i = 1,#terms do
					local term = terms[i]
					local textMatches = searchByTextProperties(term, 25)
					local scriptMatches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount = searchScriptsByText(term, {snippetLimit = 0})
					local focused = false

					if #textMatches > 0 then
						focused = focusNode(textMatches[1].Node)
					elseif #scriptMatches > 0 and nodes[scriptMatches[1]] then
						focused = focusNode(nodes[scriptMatches[1]])
					end

					local resultEntries = {}
					appendResultEntries(resultEntries, buildTextResultEntries(textMatches, 6), 10)
					if #resultEntries < 10 then
						appendResultEntries(resultEntries, buildScriptResultEntries(scriptMatches, snippetMap, math.max(0, 10 - #resultEntries), false), 10)
					end
					setResultEntries(('Text results for "%s"'):format(term), resultEntries)
					addMessage("Dex", buildTextSearchLocalText(term, textMatches, scriptMatches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, focused, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount, true), defaultMessageColor, true)
				end
			end

			assistantAi.handleTextSearchReal = function(message)
				local terms = extractTextTerms(message)

				if #terms == 0 then
					setResultEntries("Results", {})
					addMessage("Dex", 'Tell me the text to look for, like "find text sell loot" or "find text general store".', defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				for i = 1,#terms do
					local term = terms[i]
					addThinkingMessage("Dex", ('Searching visible text and script source for "%s".'):format(term), thinkingMessageColor)
					local textMatches = searchByTextProperties(term, 25)
					local scriptMatches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount = searchScriptsByText(term, {
						snippetLimit = 0,
						maxMatches = 20,
						maxScans = 160,
					})
					local focused = false

					if #textMatches > 0 then
						focused = focusNode(textMatches[1].Node)
					elseif #scriptMatches > 0 and nodes[scriptMatches[1]] then
						focused = focusNode(nodes[scriptMatches[1]])
					end

					local resultEntries = {}
					appendResultEntries(resultEntries, buildTextResultEntries(textMatches, 6), 10)
					if #resultEntries < 10 then
						appendResultEntries(resultEntries, buildScriptResultEntries(scriptMatches, snippetMap, math.max(0, 10 - #resultEntries), false), 10)
					end
					setResultEntries(('Text results for "%s"'):format(term), resultEntries)
					local context = buildTextSearchLocalText(term, textMatches, scriptMatches, scannedCount, failedCount, err, focused, truncated, totalScripts, snippetMap, elapsedSeconds, skippedDefaultCount)
					local reply, aiErr = assistantAi.callRealAi(
						message,
						"Explain these Explorer text search results clearly. Mention whether the match came from object text, script source, or both. Keep it short and practical.",
						context,
						430,
						{RequestLabel = "Asking Real ai to explain the Explorer text search"}
					)

					if reply then
						addMessage("Real ai", reply, realMessageColor)
						if type(elapsedSeconds) == "number" and elapsedSeconds > 0 then
							local formattedSeconds = elapsedSeconds >= 10 and ("%.1fs"):format(elapsedSeconds) or ("%.2fs"):format(elapsedSeconds)
							local lookupMode = assistantState.LookupMode or "Auto"
							addMessage("Dex", ("Finished in %s using %s lookup."):format(formattedSeconds, lookupMode), defaultMessageColor)
						end
					else
						addMessage("Dex", "Real ai failed on that Explorer search, so I used Dex ai instead.\n" .. tostring(aiErr), defaultMessageColor)
						addMessage("Dex", context, defaultMessageColor)
					end
				end
			end

			assistantAi.handleGenericRealPrompt = function(message)
				local reply, err = assistantAi.callRealAi(
					message,
					"Answer as a Roblox Explorer assistant. Handle simple greetings naturally. If the player wants a search, tell them to ask for a specific name, visible text, or script text. Keep the answer short.",
					buildSelectedObjectContext(),
					380,
					{RequestLabel = "Sending a general Explorer AI chat request"}
				)

				if reply then
					addMessage("Real ai", reply, realMessageColor)
				else
					addMessage("Dex", "Real ai is unavailable right now.\n" .. tostring(err), defaultMessageColor)
				end
			end

			assistantAi.isActionKeyword = function(text)
				text = lower(trim(text))
				local prefixes = {
					"can you ",
					"could you ",
					"would you ",
					"please ",
					"find ",
					"find me ",
					"list ",
					"search ",
					"show ",
					"show me ",
					"show me where ",
					"locate ",
					"look for ",
					"look up ",
					"where is ",
					"where's ",
					"wheres ",
					"bring me to ",
					"take me to ",
					"take me over to ",
					"go to ",
					"jump to ",
					"open ",
					"select ",
					"explain ",
					"explain it",
					"what does ",
					"what does it do",
					"what is ",
					"text ",
					"script ",
					"gui ",
					"ui ",
					"part ",
				}

				for i = 1,#prefixes do
					if text:find(prefixes[i], 1, true) == 1 then
						return true
					end
				end

				return false
			end

			assistantAi.splitRequests = function(message)
				local parts = {}
				local buffer = {}
				local quote = nil
				local i = 1

				while i <= #message do
					local ch = message:sub(i, i)
					if quote then
						buffer[#buffer + 1] = ch
						if ch == quote then quote = nil end
						i = i + 1
					else
						if ch == '"' or ch == "'" then
							quote = ch
							buffer[#buffer + 1] = ch
							i = i + 1
						else
							local separators = {" and ", ", ", "; ", " then ", " also "}
							local matchedSeparator = nil
							for s = 1,#separators do
								local sep = separators[s]
								if lower(message:sub(i, i + #sep - 1)) == sep and assistantAi.isActionKeyword(message:sub(i + #sep)) then
									matchedSeparator = sep
									break
								end
							end

							if matchedSeparator then
								local part = trim(table.concat(buffer))
								if part ~= "" then
									parts[#parts + 1] = part
								end
								buffer = {}
								i = i + #matchedSeparator
							else
								buffer[#buffer + 1] = ch
								i = i + 1
							end
						end
					end
				end

				local last = trim(table.concat(buffer))
				if last ~= "" then parts[#parts + 1] = last end
				return parts
			end

			assistantAi.stripGreetingPrefix = function(message)
				local updated = message
				local replacements = {
					"^[Hh]i[%s,!]+",
					"^[Hh]ello[%s,!]+",
					"^[Hh]ey[%s,!]+",
					"^[Yy]o[%s,!]+",
				}

				for i = 1,#replacements do
					local nextValue = updated:gsub(replacements[i], "", 1)
					if nextValue ~= updated then
						return trim(nextValue), true
					end
				end

				return trim(updated), false
			end

			assistantAi.normalizePromptAliases = function(message)
				local updated = trim(message)
				local previous = nil

				while updated ~= previous do
					previous = updated
					updated = updated:gsub("^[Pp]lease%s+", "", 1)
					updated = updated:gsub("^[Dd]ex[%s,:%-]+", "", 1)
					updated = updated:gsub("^[Rr]eal%s+ai[%s,:%-]+", "", 1)
					updated = updated:gsub("^[Cc]an%s+you%s+", "", 1)
					updated = updated:gsub("^[Cc]ould%s+you%s+", "", 1)
					updated = updated:gsub("^[Ww]ould%s+you%s+", "", 1)
				end

				local replacements = {
					{"^[Ss]how%s+me%s+where%s+", "bring me to "},
					{"^[Tt]ake%s+me%s+over%s+to%s+", "bring me to "},
					{"^[Bb]ring%s+me%s+over%s+to%s+", "bring me to "},
					{"^[Tt]ake%s+me%s+over%s+", "bring me to "},
					{"^[Ww]here%s+is%s+", "bring me to "},
					{"^[Ww]here's%s+", "bring me to "},
					{"^[Ww]heres%s+", "bring me to "},
					{"^[Ff]ind%s+me%s+", "find "},
					{"^[Ll]ook%s+up%s+", "find "},
					{"^[Tt]ell%s+me%s+what%s+this%s+script%s+does", "what does this script do"},
					{"^[Tt]ell%s+me%s+what%s+the%s+selected%s+script%s+does", "what does this script do"},
					{"^[Tt]ell%s+me%s+what%s+this%s+remote%s+does", "explain selected remote"},
					{"^[Tt]ell%s+me%s+what%s+the%s+selected%s+remote%s+does", "explain selected remote"},
					{"^[Tt]ell%s+me%s+what%s+it%s+does", "explain it"},
					{"^[Ww]hat%s+does%s+it%s+do", "explain it"},
				}

				for i = 1,#replacements do
					updated = updated:gsub(replacements[i][1], replacements[i][2], 1)
				end

				updated = updated:gsub("selected remotes", "selected remote")
				updated = updated:gsub("this remotes", "this remote")
				return trim(updated)
			end

			assistantAi.handleSingleRequest = function(part)
				part = assistantAi.normalizePromptAliases(part)
				local lowered = lower(trim(part))
				local usingReal = assistantState.Mode == "real"
				if lowered == "" then return end

				if lowered == "hi" or lowered == "hello" or lowered == "hey" or lowered == "yo" or lowered == "sup" or lowered == "what's up" or lowered == "whats up" then
					if usingReal then
						assistantAi.handleGenericRealPrompt(part)
					else
						addMessage("Dex", "Hi. I can search Explorer names, visible text, script source, explain the selected script or remote, and show matching results in the side panel.", defaultMessageColor)
					end
				elseif lowered == "thanks" or lowered == "thank you" or lowered == "ty" then
					addMessage("Dex", "Any time. Ask me to find text, bring you to objects, explain the selected script or remote, or show remote usage.", defaultMessageColor)
				elseif lowered == "help" or lowered:find("what can you do", 1, true) or lowered:find("how do i use", 1, true) or lowered:find("commands", 1, true) then
					addMessage("Dex", 'Try things like: "find a gui named sell loot", "find text sell loot", "bring me to remote shop", "explain selected remote", "show scripts that use it", "show children", "go to parent", "find a script that contains the word proximity", "what is selected", or "what does this script do?" Use the Lookup button at the top to cycle Auto, Executor, and Local script scanning.', defaultMessageColor)
				elseif lowered:find("what is selected", 1, true) or lowered:find("what's selected", 1, true) or lowered:find("whats selected", 1, true) then
					local primaryNode = getSelectedPrimaryNode()
					if primaryNode and primaryNode.Obj then
						setResultEntries("Selected", buildNodeResultEntries({primaryNode}, "current selection", 1))
					else
						setResultEntries("Results", {})
					end
					addMessage("Dex", getSelectedSummary(), defaultMessageColor)
				elseif lowered:find("show scripts that use it", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("show script that uses it", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("find scripts that use it", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("find script that uses it", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("bring me to the script that uses it", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("bring me to the script that fires it", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("who uses it", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("who fires it", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("listeners for it", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("handlers for it", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("show remote usage", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("find remote usage", 1, true) then
					assistantAi.handleSelectedRemoteUsageRequest(usingReal)
				elseif lowered:find("explain selected remote", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("explain this remote", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("what does this remote do", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("what does the selected remote do", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("summarize this remote", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("analyse this remote", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("analyze this remote", 1, true)
					or (lowered == "explain it" and getSelectedRemoteNode() ~= nil and getSelectedScriptNode() == nil)
					or ((lowered:find("what does this do", 1, true) or lowered:find("what does it do", 1, true)) and getSelectedRemoteNode() ~= nil and getSelectedScriptNode() == nil) then
					if usingReal then
						assistantAi.explainSelectedRemoteReal(part)
					else
						assistantAi.explainSelectedRemoteLocal()
					end
				elseif lowered:find("what does this script do", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("what does the selected script do", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("explain this script", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("summarize this script", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("analyse this script", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("analyze this script", 1, true)
					or (lowered == "explain it" and getSelectedScriptNode() ~= nil)
					or ((lowered:find("what does this do", 1, true) or lowered:find("what does it do", 1, true)) and getSelectedScriptNode() ~= nil) then
					if usingReal then
						assistantAi.explainSelectedScriptReal(part)
					else
						assistantAi.explainSelectedScriptLocal()
					end
				elseif lowered:find("script", 1, true) and (lowered:find("contain", 1, true) or lowered:find("source", 1, true) or lowered:find("code", 1, true) or lowered:find("word", 1, true) or lowered:find("text", 1, true)) then
					if usingReal then
						assistantAi.handleScriptContentSearchReal(part)
					else
						assistantAi.handleScriptContentSearchLocal(part)
					end
				elseif lowered == "parent"
					or lowered:find("go to parent", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("take me to parent", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("select parent", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("open parent", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("show parent", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("what is the parent", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("what's the parent", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("whats the parent", 1, true) then
					handleParentRequest(usingReal)
				elseif lowered:find("show children", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("list children", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("children of selected", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("children under selected", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("show .- children")
					or lowered:find("list .- children")
					or lowered:find("what is inside", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("what's inside", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("whats inside", 1, true) then
					handleChildrenRequest(part, lowered, usingReal)
				elseif lowered:find("bring me to", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("take me to", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("go to", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("jump to", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("open ", 1, true) == 1
					or lowered:find("select ", 1, true) == 1 then
					handleNavigationRequest(part, lowered, usingReal)
				elseif lowered:find("find text", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("search text", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("visible text", 1, true)
					or lowered:find("find words", 1, true)
					or (lowered:find("find", 1, true) and lowered:find("text", 1, true))
					or (lowered:find("search", 1, true) and lowered:find("text", 1, true)) then
					if usingReal then
						assistantAi.handleTextSearchReal(part)
					else
						assistantAi.handleTextSearchLocal(part)
					end
				elseif lowered:find("find", 1, true) or lowered:find("search", 1, true) or lowered:find("show", 1, true) or lowered:find("locate", 1, true) then
					if usingReal then
						assistantAi.handleNamedSearchReal(part, lowered)
					else
						assistantAi.handleNamedSearchLocal(part, lowered)
					end
				elseif usingReal then
					assistantAi.handleGenericRealPrompt(part)
				else
					addMessage("Dex", 'I did not fully understand that one. Try "help", "find text sell loot", "find a gui named sell loot", "explain selected remote", or "what does this script do?"', defaultMessageColor)
				end
			end

			handlePrompt = function()
				if requestBusy then
					addMessage("Dex", "I am still working on the last request.", defaultMessageColor)
					return
				end

				local message = trim(inputBox.Text)
				if #message == 0 then return end

				inputBox.Text = ""
				addMessage("You", message, userMessageColor)

				local strippedMessage, greeted = assistantAi.stripGreetingPrefix(message)
				if greeted and strippedMessage ~= "" then
					addMessage("Dex", "Hi. Working on that now.", defaultMessageColor)
					message = strippedMessage
				end
				message = assistantAi.normalizePromptAliases(message)
				if assistantState.Mode == "real" and assistantState.ShowThinking then
					clearThinking()
					addThinkingMessage("You", message, Color3.fromRGB(52, 76, 100))
				end

				setBusy(true)
				task.spawn(function()
					local ok, err = pcall(function()
						local parts = assistantAi.splitRequests(message)
						if #parts == 0 then
							assistantAi.handleSingleRequest(message)
						else
							for i = 1,#parts do
								assistantAi.handleSingleRequest(parts[i])
							end
						end
					end)
					if not ok then
						addMessage("Dex", ("Explorer AI hit an error: %s"):format(tostring(err)), defaultMessageColor)
					end
					setBusy(false)
				end)
			end

			assistantAi.clearCommandChips = function()
				for _, child in ipairs(chipFrame:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("TextButton") then
						child:Destroy()
					end
				end
				refreshChipCanvas()
			end

			assistantAi.addCommandChip = function(labelText, promptText)
				local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
				button.AutoButtonColor = false
				button.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(43, 52, 61)
				button.BorderSizePixel = 0
				button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22)
				button.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
				button.Text = labelText
				button.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				button.TextSize = 13
				button.Parent = chipFrame

				local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
				corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
				corner.Parent = button

				local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
				padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2)
				padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 2)
				padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
				padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
				padding.Parent = button

				button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					inputBox.Text = promptText
					handlePrompt()
				end)
			end

			assistantAi.populateCommandChips = function()
				assistantAi.clearCommandChips()
				assistantAi.addCommandChip("What is selected", "what is selected")
				assistantAi.addCommandChip("Explain script", "what does this script do?")
				assistantAi.addCommandChip("Explain remote", "explain selected remote")
				assistantAi.addCommandChip("Show remote usage", "show scripts that use it")
				assistantAi.addCommandChip("Show children", "show children")
				assistantAi.addCommandChip("Go to parent", "go to parent")
				refreshChipCanvas()
			end

			sendButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(handlePrompt)
			inputBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
				if enterPressed then
					handlePrompt()
				end
			end)

			assistantAi.populateCommandChips()
			end
			content:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
				if updateAssistantUi then
					updateAssistantUi()
				end
			end)
			updateAssistantUi()
			setAssistantMode(assistantState.Mode, true)
			addMessage("Dex", 'Explorer assistant ready. Dex ai stays local, and Real ai can use Gemini, OpenAI, Groq, or Grok once you paste that provider key. Use the top Lookup button to cycle Auto, Executor, and Local script scanning. Try the chips below, use the Results panel to jump between matches, or ask things like "explain selected remote" and "show scripts that use it".', defaultMessageColor)
			return window
		end

		assistantWindow = createExplorerAssistantWindow()

		-- Create Main Apps
		Main.CreateApp({Name = "Explorer", IconMap = Main.LargeIcons, Icon = "Explorer", Open = true, Window = Explorer.Window})

		Main.CreateApp({Name = "Properties", IconMap = Main.LargeIcons, Icon = "Properties", Open = true, Window = Properties.Window})

		Main.CreateApp({Name = "Script Viewer", IconMap = Main.LargeIcons, Icon = "Script_Viewer", Window = ScriptViewer.Window})

		local cptsOnMouseClick = nil
		local cgtsOnMouseClick = nil
		local clickPartSelectorControl, clickGuiSelectorControl

		local function disconnectClickSelect(connection)
			if connection ~= nil then
				connection:Disconnect()
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function isProtectedGuiObject(obj)
			for gui in next, Lib.ProtectedGuis do
				if gui and gui.Parent and obj:IsDescendantOf(gui) then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end

		local function getGuiObjectsAtPosition(container, x, y)
			if not container then return nil end

			local success, guiObjects = pcall(container.GetGuiObjectsAtPosition, container, x, y)
			if success and type(guiObjects) == "table" then
				return guiObjects
			end
		end

		local function findSelectableGui(guiObjects)
			if type(guiObjects) ~= "table" then return nil end

			for i = 1,#guiObjects do
				local guiObject = guiObjects[i]
				if guiObject and not isProtectedGuiObject(guiObject) then
					local current = guiObject
					while current do
						if nodes[current] then
							return current
						end
						current = current.Parent
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local function getWorldGuiSelectionTarget(part, face)
			if not part then return nil end

			local function scan(root)
				for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
						if descendant.Adornee == part or (descendant.Parent == part and descendant.Face == face) then
							if nodes[descendant] then
								return descendant
							end
						end
					elseif descendant:IsA("BillboardGui") then
						if descendant.Adornee == part or descendant.Parent == part then
							if nodes[descendant] then
								return descendant
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local current = part
			while current do
				local found = scan(current)
				if found then
					return found
				end
				current = current.Parent
			end

			return scan(workspace)
		end

		local function getGuiSelectionTarget(x, y)
			local playerGui = plr:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("PlayerGui")
			local object = findSelectableGui(getGuiObjectsAtPosition(playerGui, x, y))
			if object then
				return object
			end

			object = findSelectableGui(getGuiObjectsAtPosition(service.CoreGui, x, y))
			if object then
				return object
			end

			local mouse = Main.Mouse
			return getWorldGuiSelectionTarget(mouse.Target, mouse.TargetSurface)
		end

		clickPartSelectorControl = Main.CreateApp({Name = "Click part to select", IconMap = Main.LargeIcons, Icon = 6, OnClick = function(callback)
			if callback then
				if clickGuiSelectorControl then
					clickGuiSelectorControl.Disable()
				end
				local mouse = Main.Mouse
				cptsOnMouseClick = mouse.Button1Down:Connect(function()
					pcall(function()
						local object = mouse.Target
						if nodes[object] then
							selection:Set(nodes[object])
							Explorer.ViewNode(nodes[object])
						end
					end)
				end)
			else
				cptsOnMouseClick = disconnectClickSelect(cptsOnMouseClick)
			end
		end})

		Main.CreateApp({Name = "Console", IconMap = Main.LargeIcons, Icon = "Output", Window = Console.Window})

		Main.CreateApp({Name = "Save Instance", IconMap = Main.LargeIcons, Icon = "Watcher", Window = SaveInstance.Window})

		clickGuiSelectorControl = Main.CreateApp({Name = "Click gui to select", IconMap = Main.LargeIcons, Icon = 47, OnClick = function(callback)
			if callback then
				if clickPartSelectorControl then
					clickPartSelectorControl.Disable()
				end
				local mouse = Main.Mouse
				cgtsOnMouseClick = mouse.Button1Down:Connect(function()
					pcall(function()
						local object = getGuiSelectionTarget(mouse.X, mouse.Y)
						if object and nodes[object] then
							selection:Set(nodes[object])
							Explorer.ViewNode(nodes[object])
						end
					end)
				end)
			else
				cgtsOnMouseClick = disconnectClickSelect(cgtsOnMouseClick)
			end
		end})

		Main.CreateApp({Name = "Explorer AI", IconMap = Main.LargeIcons, Icon = "Script_Viewer", Window = assistantWindow})

		Lib.ShowGui(gui)
	end

	Main.SetupFilesystem = function()
		if not env.writefile or not env.makefolder then return end
		local writefile, makefolder = env.writefile, env.makefolder
		makefolder("dex")
		makefolder("dex/assets")
		makefolder("dex/saved")
		makefolder("dex/plugins")
		makefolder("dex/ModuleCache")
	end

	Main.LocalDepsUpToDate = function()
		return Main.DepsVersionData and Main.ClientVersion == Main.DepsVersionData[1]
	end

	Main.Init = function()
		Main.Elevated = pcall(function() local a = service.CoreGui:GetFullName() end)
		Main.InitEnv()
		Main.LoadSettings()
		Main.SetupFilesystem()

		-- Load Lib
		local intro = Main.CreateIntro("Initializing Library")
		Lib = Main.LoadModule("Lib")
		Lib.FastWait()

		-- Init other stuff
		--Main.IncompatibleTest()

		-- Init icons
		Main.MiscIcons = Lib.IconMap.new("http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=6511490623",256,256,16,16) -- 6579106223

		Main.MiscIcons:SetDict({
			["Reference"] = 0;
			["Cut"] = 1;
			["Cut_Disabled"] = 2;
			["Copy"] = 3;
			["Copy_Disabled"] = 4;
			["Paste"] = 5;
			["Paste_Disabled"] = 6;
			["Delete"] = 7;
			["Delete_Disabled"] = 8;
			["Group"] = 9;
			["Group_Disabled"] = 10;
			["Ungroup"] = 11;
			["Ungroup_Disabled"] = 12;
			["TeleportTo"] = 13;
			["Rename"] = 14;
			["JumpToParent"] = 15;
			["ExploreData"] = 16;
			["Save"] = 17;
			["CallFunction"] = 18;
			["CallRemote"] = 19;
			["Undo"] = 20,
			["Undo_Disabled"] = 21;
			["Redo"] = 22;
			["Redo_Disabled"] = 23;
			["Expand_Over"] = 24;
			["Expand"] = 25;
			["Collapse_Over"] = 26;
			["Collapse"] = 27;
			["SelectChildren"] = 28;
			["SelectChildren_Disabled"] = 29;
			["InsertObject"] = 30;
			["ViewScript"] = 31;
			["AddStar"] = 32;
			["RemoveStar"] = 33;
			["Script_Disabled"] = 34;
			["LocalScript_Disabled"] = 35;
			["Play"] = 36;
			["Pause"] = 37;
			["Rename_Disabled"] = 38;
		})
		Main.LargeIcons = Lib.IconMap.new("rbxassetid://6579106223",256,256,32,32)
		Main.LargeIcons:SetDict({
			Explorer = 0, Properties = 1, Script_Viewer = 2, Watcher = 3, Output = 4
		})

		-- Fetch version if needed
		intro.SetProgress("Fetching Roblox Version",0.2)
		if Main.Elevated then
			local fileVer = Lib.ReadFile("dex/deps_version.dat")
			Main.ClientVersion = Version()

			if fileVer then
				Main.DepsVersionData = string.split(fileVer,"\n")
				if Main.LocalDepsUpToDate() then
					Main.RobloxVersion = Main.DepsVersionData[2]
				end
			end
			Main.RobloxVersion = Main.RobloxVersion or oldgame:HttpGet("http://setup.roblox.com/versionQTStudio")

			-- backup for kaboom
			if #Main.RobloxVersion < 1 then
				Main.RobloxVersion = oldgame:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/infyiff/backup/refs/heads/main/deps_version.dat"):gsub("%s+", "")
			end
		end

		-- Fetch external deps
		intro.SetProgress("Fetching API",0.35)
		API = Main.FetchAPI()
		Lib.FastWait()
		intro.SetProgress("Fetching RMD",0.5)
		RMD = Main.FetchRMD()
		Lib.FastWait()

		-- Save external deps locally if needed
		if Main.Elevated and env.writefile and not Main.LocalDepsUpToDate() then
			env.writefile("dex/deps_version.dat",Main.ClientVersion.."\n"..Main.RobloxVersion)
			env.writefile("dex/rbx_api.dat",Main.RawAPI)
			env.writefile("dex/rbx_rmd.dat",Main.RawRMD)
		end

		-- Load other modules
		intro.SetProgress("Loading Modules",0.75)
		Main.AppControls.Lib.InitDeps(Main.GetInitDeps()) -- Missing deps now available
		Main.LoadModules()
		Lib.FastWait()

		-- Init other modules
		intro.SetProgress("Initializing Modules",0.9)
		Explorer.Init()
		Properties.Init()
		ScriptViewer.Init()
		Console.Init()
		SaveInstance.Init()
		Lib.FastWait()

		-- Done
		intro.SetProgress("Complete",1)
		coroutine.wrap(function()
			Lib.FastWait(1.25)
			intro.Close()
		end)()

		-- Init window system, create main menu, show explorer and properties
		Lib.Window.Init()
		Main.CreateMainGui()
		Explorer.Window:Show({Align = "right", Pos = 1, Size = 0.5, Silent = true})
		Properties.Window:Show({Align = "right", Pos = 2, Size = 0.5, Silent = true})
		Lib.DeferFunc(function() Lib.Window.ToggleSide("right") end)
	end

	return Main
end)()

-- Start
Main.Init()
